===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "frametag/rename.py", "role": "current_project", "bytes": 21410, "sha256": "8f194793317a3b6e574d062207dfe8f7cd3b368ac4dc1913eb592762733efa9b"} =====
"""No-clobber filename transactions with crash recovery and persistent undo."""
from __future__ import annotations

from contextlib import contextmanager
from dataclasses import replace
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import stat
from typing import Any, Iterator
import uuid

from .domain import sanitize_filename
from .models import Photo
from .storage import Cache, atomic_json, photo_from_dict, photo_to_dict


class RenameError(RuntimeError):
    pass


_PENDING = ".frametag_pending_v2.json"
_HISTORY = "frametag_history_v2.json"
_LOCK = ".frametag_v2.lock"


@contextmanager
def _operation_lock(root: Path) -> Iterator[None]:
    """OS locks release automatically if the application terminates."""
    with (root / _LOCK).open("a+b") as stream:
        if stream.tell() == 0:
            stream.write(b"\0")
            stream.flush()
        stream.seek(0)
        try:
            if os.name == "nt":
                import msvcrt
                msvcrt.locking(stream.fileno(), msvcrt.LK_NBLCK, 1)
            else:
                import fcntl
                fcntl.flock(stream.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except OSError as exc:
            raise RenameError("다른 FrameTag 작업이 이 폴더의 이름을 변경하고 있습니다. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.") from exc
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            stream.seek(0)
            if os.name == "nt":
                import msvcrt
                msvcrt.locking(stream.fileno(), msvcrt.LK_UNLCK, 1)
            else:
                import fcntl
                fcntl.flock(stream.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_UN)


def _root(root: Path, cache: Cache) -> Path:
    root = Path(root).resolve()
    if not root.is_dir() or cache.root != root:
        raise RenameError("선택한 사진 폴더와 캐시 폴더가 일치하지 않습니다.")
    return root


def _relative(root: Path, path: Path) -> str:
    try:
        return str(path.relative_to(root))
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise RenameError("선택한 폴더 밖의 사진은 변경할 수 없습니다.") from exc


def _path(root: Path, value: str) -> Path:
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        raise RenameError("복구 기록의 경로 형식이 올바르지 않습니다.")
    relative = Path(value)
    if relative.is_absolute() or ".." in relative.parts or not relative.parts:
        raise RenameError("복구 기록에 안전하지 않은 경로가 있습니다.")
    path = root / relative
    _relative(root, path.resolve())
    return path


def _fingerprint(path: Path) -> dict[str, int]:
    try:
        info = path.lstat()
    except OSError as exc:
        raise RenameError(f"사진 파일을 확인할 수 없습니다: {path.name}. 파일 존재 여부와 접근 권한을 확인해 주세요.") from exc
    if not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode):
        raise RenameError("일반 사진 파일만 이름을 변경할 수 있습니다.")
    return {"size": info.st_size, "mtime_ns": info.st_mtime_ns, "dev": info.st_dev, "ino": info.st_ino}


def _matches(path: Path, entry: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    try:
        return _fingerprint(path) == {key: entry[key] for key in ("size", "mtime_ns", "dev", "ino")}
    except (OSError, RenameError):
        return False


def _exists(path: Path) -> bool:
    return os.path.lexists(path)


def _move_no_clobber(source: Path, target: Path, entry: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Use Windows' exclusive rename or POSIX exclusive link; never replace a file."""
    if not _matches(source, entry):
        raise RenameError(f"파일이 변경되었습니다. 다시 확인해 주세요: {source.name}")
    if os.name == "nt":
        try:
            # Unlike POSIX rename, Windows os.rename fails if destination exists.
            # This also supports photo folders on exFAT/FAT32 removable drives.
            os.rename(source, target)
        except FileExistsError as exc:
            raise RenameError(f"같은 이름의 파일이 생겨 변경을 중단했습니다: {target.name}") from exc
        except OSError as exc:
            raise RenameError(f"파일 이름을 변경하지 못했습니다: {source.name}. 파일 사용 여부와 폴더 권한을 확인해 주세요.") from exc
        if not _matches(target, entry):
            raise RenameError(f"변경 중 파일 내용이 바뀌었습니다: {source.name}")
        return
    try:
        os.link(source, target, follow_symlinks=False)
    except FileExistsError as exc:
        raise RenameError(f"같은 이름의 파일이 생겨 변경을 중단했습니다: {target.name}") from exc
    except OSError as exc:
        raise RenameError(
            f"안전하게 파일 이름을 변경하지 못했습니다: {source.name}. "
            "파일 사용 여부, 폴더 권한, 하드 링크를 지원하는 파일시스템인지 확인해 주세요."
        ) from exc
    if not _matches(target, entry) or not _matches(source, entry):
        # Remove only the link we just created if its identity still matches the source.
        try:
            if os.path.samefile(source, target):
                target.unlink()
        except OSError:
            pass
        raise RenameError(f"변경 중 파일 내용이 바뀌었습니다: {source.name}")
    try:
        source.unlink()
    except OSError:
        # The original still exists. Discard only our own matching destination link.
        if _matches(source, entry) and _matches(target, entry):
            target.unlink()
        raise


def _read_json(path: Path, default: Any) -> Any:
    try:
        with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            return json.load(stream)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return default
    except (ValueError, UnicodeError) as exc:
        raise RenameError(f"기록 파일을 읽지 못했습니다. 파일을 보존하고 확인해 주세요: {path.name}") from exc


def _history(root: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    history = _read_json(root / _HISTORY, [])
    if not isinstance(history, list) or any(not isinstance(item, dict) for item in history):
        raise RenameError("이름 변경 기록의 형식이 올바르지 않습니다.")
    return history


def _validate_transaction(root: Path, transaction: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(transaction, dict) or transaction.get("version") != 2:
        raise RenameError("복구 기록의 버전이 올바르지 않습니다.")
    transaction_id = transaction.get("id")
    try:
        if uuid.UUID(hex=transaction_id).hex != transaction_id:
            raise ValueError
    except (ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
        raise RenameError("복구 기록의 작업 번호가 올바르지 않습니다.") from exc
    if transaction.get("kind") not in {"rename", "undo"} or transaction.get("phase") not in {
        "prepared", "staging", "committing", "files_committed", "metadata_done", "rolling_back"
    }:
        raise RenameError("복구 기록의 작업 상태가 올바르지 않습니다.")
    entries = transaction.get("entries")
    if not isinstance(entries, list):
        raise RenameError("복구 기록의 파일 목록이 올바르지 않습니다.")
    seen: set[Path] = set()
    for index, entry in enumerate(entries):
        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
            raise RenameError("복구 기록의 파일 정보가 올바르지 않습니다.")
        try:
            source = _path(root, entry["source"])
            target = _path(root, entry["target"])
            temporary = _path(root, entry["temporary"])
            if source.parent != target.parent or temporary != source.parent / f".frametag-{transaction_id}-{index}.tmp":
                raise RenameError("복구 기록의 임시 파일 경로가 올바르지 않습니다.")
            if source in seen or str(source) == str(target):
                raise RenameError("복구 기록에 중복된 파일이 있습니다.")
            seen.add(source)
            if any(not isinstance(entry[key], int) for key in ("size", "mtime_ns", "dev", "ino")):
                raise ValueError
            photo_from_dict(entry["before"])
            photo_from_dict(entry["after"])
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise RenameError("복구 기록의 사진 정보가 올바르지 않습니다.") from exc
    return transaction


def _records(root: Path, transaction: dict[str, Any], reverse: bool = False) -> list[tuple[Path, Path, Photo]]:
    records = []
    for entry in transaction["entries"]:
        source = _path(root, entry["source"])
        target = _path(root, entry["target"])
        photo = photo_from_dict(entry["before"] if reverse else entry["after"])
        photo.path = source if reverse else target
        if reverse:
            records.append((target, source, photo))
        else:
            records.append((source, target, photo))
    if not reverse:
        for data in transaction.get("stationary", []):
            photo = photo_from_dict(data)
            _relative(root, photo.path.resolve())
            records.append((photo.path, photo.path, photo))
    return records


def _finish(root: Path, cache: Cache, transaction: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    for entry in transaction["entries"]:
        if not _matches(_path(root, entry["target"]), entry):
            raise RenameError("변경된 사진이 기록과 다릅니다. 복구 기록을 보존하고 파일을 확인해 주세요.")
    history = _history(root)
    cache.relocate_many(_records(root, transaction))
    if transaction["kind"] == "rename":
        if not any(item.get("id") == transaction["id"] for item in history):
            history.append({"id": transaction["id"], "entries": transaction["entries"]})
    else:
        # Idempotent when a previous attempt saved history before terminating.
        history = [item for item in history if item.get("id") != transaction["undo_id"]]
    atomic_json(root / _HISTORY, history[-50:])
    transaction["phase"] = "metadata_done"
    atomic_json(root / _PENDING, transaction)
    (root / _PENDING).unlink()


def _rollback(root: Path, cache: Cache, transaction: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Stage every original inode before restoring names, including name-swap cycles."""
    entries = transaction["entries"]
    for entry in entries:
        paths = {_path(root, entry[key]) for key in ("source", "temporary", "target")}
        if not any(_matches(path, entry) for path in paths):
            raise RenameError("원래 사진을 안전하게 찾지 못했습니다. 임시 파일과 복구 기록을 보존해 주세요.")
    # Never begin recovery if an unrelated file occupies an original name.
    for entry in entries:
        source = _path(root, entry["source"])
        if _exists(source) and not any(_matches(source, candidate) for candidate in entries):
            raise RenameError(f"원래 이름에 다른 파일이 있어 복구를 중단했습니다: {source.name}")
    transaction["phase"] = "rolling_back"
    atomic_json(root / _PENDING, transaction)
    for entry in entries:
        temporary = _path(root, entry["temporary"])
        if not _matches(temporary, entry):
            candidates = [_path(root, entry[key]) for key in ("source", "target")]
            source = next((path for path in candidates if _matches(path, entry)), None)
            if source is None:
                raise RenameError("사진 복구 위치를 찾을 수 없습니다.")
            _move_no_clobber(source, temporary, entry)
        # A crash between link and unlink can leave two copies of the same inode.
        for key in ("source", "target"):
            path = _path(root, entry[key])
            if path != temporary and _matches(path, entry):
                path.unlink()
    for entry in entries:
        source = _path(root, entry["source"])
        _move_no_clobber(_path(root, entry["temporary"]), source, entry)
    cache.relocate_many(_records(root, transaction, reverse=True))
    (root / _PENDING).unlink()


def _recover_pending(root: Path, cache: Cache) -> None:
    transaction = _read_json(root / _PENDING, None)
    if transaction is None:
        return
    transaction = _validate_transaction(root, transaction)
    if transaction["phase"] in {"files_committed", "metadata_done"}:
        _finish(root, cache, transaction)
    else:
        _rollback(root, cache, transaction)


def recover_pending(root: Path, cache: Cache) -> None:
    """Complete committed metadata or safely undo an interrupted file transaction."""
    root = _root(root, cache)
    with _operation_lock(root):
        _recover_pending(root, cache)


def _execute(root: Path, cache: Cache, transaction: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    atomic_json(root / _PENDING, transaction)
    try:
        transaction["phase"] = "staging"
        atomic_json(root / _PENDING, transaction)
        for entry in transaction["entries"]:
            _move_no_clobber(_path(root, entry["source"]), _path(root, entry["temporary"]), entry)
        transaction["phase"] = "committing"
        atomic_json(root / _PENDING, transaction)
        for entry in transaction["entries"]:
            _move_no_clobber(_path(root, entry["temporary"]), _path(root, entry["target"]), entry)
        transaction["phase"] = "files_committed"
        atomic_json(root / _PENDING, transaction)
    except Exception as exc:
        try:
            _rollback(root, cache, transaction)
        except Exception as rollback_error:
            raise RenameError(
                "이름 변경을 완료하지 못했고 자동 복구도 중단되었습니다. 사진과 임시 파일, "
                "복구 기록을 보존한 뒤 다시 시도해 주세요. " + str(rollback_error)
            ) from exc
        raise RenameError("이름 변경을 완료하지 못해 원래 이름으로 복구했습니다. " + str(exc)) from exc
    try:
        _finish(root, cache, transaction)
    except Exception as exc:
        raise RenameError(
            "사진 이름은 변경되었지만 기록 저장이 완료되지 않았습니다. "
            "복구 기록을 삭제하지 말고 폴더를 다시 열어 기록 저장을 재시도해 주세요."
        ) from exc


def _copy_photo(target: Photo, source: Photo) -> None:
    for key, value in vars(source).items():
        setattr(target, key, value)


def apply_names(root: Path, photos: list[Photo], cache: Cache) -> list[tuple[Path, Path]]:
    root = _root(root, cache)
    if not photos:
        return []
    with _operation_lock(root):
        _recover_pending(root, cache)
        _history(root)  # Validate undo history before touching photo files.
        sources: set[Path] = set()
        identities: set[tuple[int, int]] = set()
        prepared: list[tuple[Photo, Path, str, dict[str, int]]] = []
        for photo in photos:
            raw_path = Path(photo.path)
            if raw_path.is_symlink():
                raise RenameError("바로가기나 심볼릭 링크는 변경할 수 없습니다.")
            source = raw_path.resolve()
            _relative(root, source)
            if source in sources:
                raise RenameError("같은 사진이 두 번 선택되었습니다.")
            fingerprint = _fingerprint(source)
            identity = (fingerprint["dev"], fingerprint["ino"])
            if identity in identities:
                raise RenameError("같은 파일을 가리키는 하드 링크가 여러 개 선택되었습니다. 한 파일씩 선택해 주세요.")
            identities.add(identity)
            if fingerprint["size"] != photo.size or fingerprint["mtime_ns"] != photo.mtime_ns:
                raise RenameError(f"분석 후 파일 내용이 바뀌었습니다. 다시 분석해 주세요: {source.name}")
            try:
                filename = sanitize_filename(photo.proposed_name, source.suffix)
            except ValueError as exc:
                raise RenameError(str(exc)) from exc
            sources.add(source)
            prepared.append((photo, source, filename, fingerprint))
        occupied: dict[Path, set[str]] = {}
        for parent in {source.parent for source in sources}:
            occupied[parent] = {item.name.casefold() for item in parent.iterdir() if item not in sources}
        transaction_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        entries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        after_photos: list[tuple[Photo, Photo]] = []
        stationary: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        next_suffix: dict[tuple[Path, str], int] = {}
        for photo, source, filename, fingerprint in prepared:
            names = occupied[source.parent]
            candidate = filename
            stem = Path(filename).stem
            suffix = Path(filename).suffix
            suffix_key = (source.parent, filename.casefold())
            duplicate = next_suffix.get(suffix_key, 2)
            while candidate.casefold() in names:
                ending = f"_{duplicate}{suffix}"
                budget = 240 - len(ending.encode("utf-8"))
                trimmed = stem.encode("utf-8")[:budget].decode("utf-8", errors="ignore").rstrip(" .")
                candidate = sanitize_filename(f"{trimmed}{ending}", source.suffix)
                duplicate += 1
            next_suffix[suffix_key] = duplicate
            names.add(candidate.casefold())
            target = source.with_name(candidate)
            after = replace(photo, path=target, proposed_name=candidate, confirmed=True, status="확인 완료", error="", revision=photo.revision + 1)
            after_photos.append((photo, after))
            if str(target) == str(source):
                stationary.append(photo_to_dict(after))
                continue
            entries.append({
                "source": _relative(root, source), "target": _relative(root, target),
                "temporary": _relative(root, source.parent / f".frametag-{transaction_id}-{len(entries)}.tmp"),
                **fingerprint, "before": photo_to_dict(photo), "after": photo_to_dict(after),
            })
        if not entries:
            cache.put_many([after for _, after in after_photos])
        else:
            transaction = {"version": 2, "id": transaction_id, "kind": "rename", "phase": "prepared", "entries": entries, "stationary": stationary}
            _execute(root, cache, transaction)
        for original, after in after_photos:
            _copy_photo(original, after)
        return [(_path(root, entry["source"]), _path(root, entry["target"])) for entry in entries]


def undo_last(root: Path, cache: Cache) -> list[tuple[Path, Path]]:
    root = _root(root, cache)
    with _operation_lock(root):
        _recover_pending(root, cache)
        history = _history(root)
        if not history:
            return []
        previous = history[-1]
        if not isinstance(previous.get("entries"), list) or not previous.get("id"):
            raise RenameError("최근 이름 변경 기록의 형식이 올바르지 않습니다.")
        transaction_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        entries = []
        for index, prior in enumerate(previous["entries"]):
            try:
                source = _path(root, prior["target"])
                target = _path(root, prior["source"])
                fingerprint = _fingerprint(source)
                if fingerprint != {key: prior[key] for key in ("size", "mtime_ns", "dev", "ino")}:
                    raise RenameError(f"이름 변경 후 사진 내용이 바뀌어 되돌릴 수 없습니다: {source.name}")
                before = cache.get(source) or photo_from_dict(prior["after"])
                after = photo_from_dict(prior["before"])
                after.path = target
                after.revision = max(after.revision, before.revision) + 1
                if source.parent != target.parent:
                    raise RenameError("이름 변경 기록의 폴더가 올바르지 않습니다.")
                entries.append({
                    "source": _relative(root, source), "target": _relative(root, target),
                    "temporary": _relative(root, source.parent / f".frametag-{transaction_id}-{index}.tmp"),
                    **fingerprint, "before": photo_to_dict(before), "after": photo_to_dict(after),
                })
            except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
                raise RenameError("이름 변경 기록의 사진 정보가 올바르지 않습니다.") from exc
        sources = {_path(root, entry["source"]) for entry in entries}
        occupied = {
            parent: {item.name.casefold() for item in parent.iterdir() if item not in sources}
            for parent in {source.parent for source in sources}
        }
        for entry in entries:
            target = _path(root, entry["target"])
            if target.name.casefold() in occupied[target.parent]:
                raise RenameError(f"원래 이름에 다른 파일이 있어 되돌릴 수 없습니다: {target.name}")
        transaction = {
            "version": 2, "id": transaction_id, "kind": "undo", "phase": "prepared",
            "entries": entries, "undo_id": previous["id"], "stationary": [],
        }
        _validate_transaction(root, transaction)
        _execute(root, cache, transaction)
        return [(_path(root, entry["source"]), _path(root, entry["target"])) for entry in entries]

===== END FILE {"path": "frametag/rename.py"} =====

===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "frametag/engine.py", "role": "current_project", "bytes": 33625, "sha256": "cf956ed48d816ac308c4e2f95588df84248561ca9c7b2620712e2038d0db20b8"} =====
"""Sequential analysis and persistent, copy-safe folder watching.

The callback receives immutable-by-convention snapshots and never touches a GUI.
A scanner discovers arrivals during API waits. All API calls run on one
cancellable worker; scans and rename operations share its lock.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import json
import re
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable

from PIL import Image

from .api import APIError, Analyzer, Cancelled
from .domain import (filename_from_analysis, is_gender_prefixed_filename, list_images, natural_key,
                     normalize_analysis, parse_frametag_filename,
                     sanitize_filename, select_through, unify_runs)
from .models import Photo
from .rename import apply_names, recover_pending, undo_last
from .storage import Cache, atomic_json


class Engine:
    def __init__(self, callback: Callable[[str, Any], None], interval: float = 1.0,
                 stable_scans: int = 3, analyzer_factory: Callable[..., Any] = Analyzer):
        self._callback = callback
        self._interval = max(0.02, float(interval))
        self._stable_scans = max(2, int(stable_scans))
        self._factory = analyzer_factory
        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._wake = threading.Event()
        self._watch_stop = threading.Event()
        self._cancel = threading.Event()
        self._closed = False
        self._busy = False
        self._operation = ""
        self._manual_requested = False
        self._auto = False
        self._config = ("", "", "")
        self._root: Path | None = None
        self._recursive = False
        self._cache: Cache | None = None
        self._photos: dict[str, Photo] = {}
        self._known: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
        self._stable: dict[str, tuple[tuple[int, int], int]] = {}
        self._last_poll = 0.0
        self._consecutive_failures = 0
        self._manifest_error = ""
        self._worker = threading.Thread(target=self._run, name="FrameTag-worker", daemon=True)
        self._watcher = threading.Thread(target=self._run_watch, name="FrameTag-watch", daemon=True)
        self._worker.start()
        self._watcher.start()

    def _emit(self, event: str, value: Any) -> None:
        self._callback(event, value)

    def _ordered(self) -> list[Photo]:
        return sorted(self._photos.values(), key=lambda p: (
            not (p.confirmed or is_gender_prefixed_filename(p.path.name)),
            natural_key(str(p.path.parent)), natural_key(p.path.name)))

    def get_photos(self) -> list[Photo]:
        with self._lock:
            return copy.deepcopy(self._ordered())

    def get_root(self) -> Path | None:
        with self._lock:
            return self._root

    def is_busy(self) -> bool:
        with self._lock:
            return self._busy

    def _publish(self) -> None:
        self._emit("photos", copy.deepcopy(self._ordered()))

    def _key(self, path: Path) -> str:
        assert self._root is not None
        return path.relative_to(self._root).as_posix()

    @staticmethod
    def _fingerprint(path: Path) -> tuple[int, int]:
        stat = path.stat()
        return stat.st_size, stat.st_mtime_ns

    @staticmethod
    def _record_fingerprint(record: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, int]:
        return record.get("size", -1), record.get("mtime_ns", -1)

    @staticmethod
    def _record(photo: Photo, state: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"size": photo.size, "mtime_ns": photo.mtime_ns,
                "state": state, "present": True}

    def _save_manifest(self) -> None:
        if self._root is None:
            return
        try:
            atomic_json(self._root / ".frametag_watch_v2.json", {
                "version": 2, "recursive": self._recursive, "entries": self._known})
            self._manifest_error = ""
        except OSError as exc:
            message = f"폴더 감시 기록을 저장하지 못했습니다: {exc}"
            if message != self._manifest_error:
                self._emit("error", message)
            self._manifest_error = message

    def _load_manifest(self, root: Path) -> tuple[dict[str, dict[str, Any]], bool, bool]:
        path = root / ".frametag_watch_v2.json"
        if not path.exists():
            return {}, True, False
        try:
            data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if data.get("version") != 2 or not isinstance(data.get("entries"), dict):
                raise ValueError("알 수 없는 감시 기록 형식")
            entries = data["entries"]
            for name, record in entries.items():
                if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(record, dict):
                    raise ValueError("손상된 감시 기록")
                if record.get("state") not in {"baseline", "pending", "error", "processed"}:
                    raise ValueError("손상된 감시 상태")
            return entries, False, bool(data.get("recursive", False))
        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
            # Preserve a malformed local file before establishing a safe new baseline.
            backup = path.with_name(f"{path.name}.broken-{time.time_ns()}")
            try:
                path.rename(backup)
            except OSError:
                raise ValueError(f"감시 기록을 읽거나 보존할 수 없습니다: {exc}") from exc
            self._emit("error", f"감시 기록이 손상되어 원본을 {backup.name}에 보존했습니다.")
            return {}, True, False

    def load_folder(self, root: Path, recursive: bool = False) -> bool:
        with self._lock:
            if self._busy or self._closed:
                self._emit("error", "분석이나 파일 변경이 끝난 후 폴더를 불러오세요.")
                return False
            root = Path(root).expanduser().resolve()
            if not root.is_dir():
                self._emit("error", "사진 폴더가 존재하지 않습니다.")
                return False
            old_state = (self._root, self._recursive, self._cache, self._known,
                         self._photos, self._stable, self._last_poll,
                         self._consecutive_failures)
            self._busy = True
            self._emit("busy", True)
            try:
                cache = Cache(root)
                recover_pending(root, cache)
                known, first_baseline, previous_recursive = self._load_manifest(root)
                self._root, self._recursive, self._cache = root, bool(recursive), cache
                self._known, self._photos, self._stable = known, {}, {}
                self._last_poll = 0.0
                self._consecutive_failures = 0
                self._poll(first_baseline=first_baseline,
                           baseline_subfolders=bool(recursive) and not previous_recursive)
                self._emit("status", f"사진 {len(self._photos):,}장을 불러왔습니다.")
            except Exception as exc:
                (self._root, self._recursive, self._cache, self._known,
                 self._photos, self._stable, self._last_poll,
                 self._consecutive_failures) = old_state
                if self._auto:
                    self._auto = False
                    self._emit("auto", False)
                self._publish()
                self._emit("error", f"폴더를 불러오지 못했습니다: {exc}")
                return False
            finally:
                self._busy = False
                self._emit("busy", False)
        self._wake.set()
        return True

    def configure(self, provider: str, model: str, key: str) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            config = (provider, model, key)
            if config != self._config:
                self._config = config
                self._consecutive_failures = 0
                # A corrected provider/key/model can resume outstanding error photos.
                changed = False
                for record in self._known.values():
                    if record.get("state") == "error":
                        record["state"] = "pending"
                        changed = True
                if changed:
                    self._save_manifest()
        self._wake.set()

    def set_auto(self, enabled: bool) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            enabled = bool(enabled) and not self._closed
            if enabled == self._auto:
                return
            self._auto = enabled
            if not enabled and self._operation == "auto":
                self._cancel.set()
            self._emit("auto", enabled)
            self._emit("status", "새 사진 자동 감시를 시작합니다." if enabled else "자동 감시를 중지했습니다.")
        self._wake.set()

    def start_analysis(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            if self._closed or self._busy:
                return
            if self._root is None:
                self._emit("error", "먼저 사진 폴더를 선택하세요.")
                return
            if not all(self._config):
                self._emit("error", "API 공급자, 모델과 API 키를 설정하세요.")
                return
            self._cancel = threading.Event()
            self._busy, self._operation, self._manual_requested = True, "manual", True
            self._emit("busy", True)
        self._wake.set()

    def stop_analysis(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._cancel.set()
            if self._auto:
                self._auto = False
                self._emit("auto", False)
            self._emit("status", "분석 중지를 요청했습니다.")
        self._wake.set()

    @staticmethod
    def _needs_analysis(photo: Photo) -> bool:
        return (not photo.confirmed and not photo.manual
                and not is_gender_prefixed_filename(photo.path.name)) and (
            not photo.analysis or not photo.proposed_name
            or (bool(photo.error) and photo.status != "파일명 오류"))

    def _poll(self, first_baseline: bool = False, baseline_subfolders: bool = False) -> None:
        """Called under the shared lock; size/mtime stability is measured per scan."""
        if self._root is None or self._cache is None:
            return
        self._last_poll = time.monotonic()
        paths = list_images(self._root, self._recursive)
        present: set[str] = set()
        changed, manifest_changed = False, False
        recognized: list[Photo] = []
        observed = {}
        for path in paths:
            path = path.resolve()
            try:
                fingerprint = self._fingerprint(path)
            except (FileNotFoundError, PermissionError, OSError):
                continue
            observed[path] = fingerprint
        changed_paths = {path: fingerprint for path, fingerprint in observed.items()
                         if str(path) not in self._photos
                         or (self._photos[str(path)].size, self._photos[str(path)].mtime_ns) != fingerprint}
        cached = self._cache.get_many(changed_paths)
        for path, fingerprint in observed.items():
            name, identifier = self._key(path), str(path)
            present.add(name)
            previous_fingerprint, count = self._stable.get(name, (fingerprint, 0))
            self._stable[name] = (fingerprint, count + 1 if previous_fingerprint == fingerprint else 1)
            photo = self._photos.get(identifier)
            if photo is None or (photo.size, photo.mtime_ns) != fingerprint:
                # A matching cache owns the user's edits and undo state. Only
                # infer completion from a filename when no valid record exists.
                photo = cached.get(identifier)
                if photo is None:
                    analysis = parse_frametag_filename(path.name)
                    photo = Photo(path, *fingerprint)
                    if analysis is not None:
                        photo.analysis = analysis
                        photo.proposed_name = path.name
                        photo.confirmed = True
                        photo.status = "기존 이름 변경 완료"
                        recognized.append(photo)
                self._photos[identifier] = photo
                changed = True
            previous = self._known.get(name)
            excluded = is_gender_prefixed_filename(path.name)
            if (previous is None or self._record_fingerprint(previous) != fingerprint
                    or not previous.get("present", True)
                    or ((photo.confirmed or photo.manual or excluded)
                        and previous.get("state") != "processed")):
                added_scope_baseline = baseline_subfolders and previous is None and "/" in name
                state = "baseline" if first_baseline or added_scope_baseline else "pending"
                if photo.confirmed or photo.manual or excluded:
                    state = "processed"
                self._known[name] = self._record(photo, state)
                manifest_changed = True
        for identifier, photo in list(self._photos.items()):
            if self._key(photo.path) not in present:
                del self._photos[identifier]
                changed = True
        for name, record in self._known.items():
            # Excluded subfolders are outside this scan's scope, rather than deleted.
            if not self._recursive and "/" in name:
                continue
            if name not in present and record.get("present", True):
                record["present"] = False
                self._stable.pop(name, None)
                manifest_changed = True
        if recognized:
            # Recognition stores existing metadata without creating a rename
            # transaction or charging an analysis request, including on restart.
            self._cache.put_many(recognized)
        if manifest_changed:
            self._save_manifest()
        if changed:
            self._publish()

    def _ready_auto_photo(self) -> str | None:
        for photo in self._ordered():
            name = self._key(photo.path)
            record = self._known.get(name, {})
            if (record.get("state") != "pending" or photo.confirmed or photo.manual
                    or is_gender_prefixed_filename(photo.path.name)):
                continue
            fingerprint, count = self._stable.get(name, ((-1, -1), 0))
            if count < self._stable_scans or fingerprint[0] <= 0:
                continue
            # A copy may pause without finishing: never charge for an undecodable file.
            try:
                with Image.open(photo.path) as image:
                    # JPEG verify() checks headers only; load() also rejects a paused/truncated copy.
                    image.load()
                if self._fingerprint(photo.path) != fingerprint:
                    continue
            except (OSError, ValueError, Image.DecompressionBombError):
                continue
            return str(photo.path)
        return None

    def _mark(self, photo: Photo, state: str) -> None:
        self._known[self._key(photo.path)] = self._record(photo, state)
        self._save_manifest()

    def _pause_for_failure(self, message: str) -> None:
        if self._auto:
            self._auto = False
            self._emit("auto", False)
        self._emit("error", message)

    def _process(self, identifier: str, automatic: bool) -> str:
        with self._lock:
            photo = self._photos.get(identifier)
            if photo is None or self._cancel.is_set():
                return "cancelled"
            if is_gender_prefixed_filename(photo.path.name):
                return "excluded"
            snapshot = copy.deepcopy(photo)
            config, cancel = self._config, self._cancel
            cache = self._cache
            assert cache is not None
            reuse = automatic and bool(photo.analysis and photo.proposed_name
                                       and (not photo.error or photo.status == "파일명 오류"))
            if not reuse:
                photo.status = "분석 중"
                photo.error = ""
                self._publish()
                self._emit("status", f"{'자동 ' if automatic else ''}분석: {photo.path.name}")
        try:
            if cancel.is_set():
                raise Cancelled()
            if reuse:
                analysis = snapshot.analysis
            else:
                if not all(config):
                    raise APIError("API 공급자, 모델과 API 키를 설정하세요.", fatal=True)
                analysis = normalize_analysis(self._factory(*config).analyze(snapshot.path, cancel))
            if cancel.is_set():
                raise Cancelled()
            with self._lock:
                photo = self._photos.get(identifier)
                if photo is None:
                    return "stale"
                if (photo.size, photo.mtime_ns) != (snapshot.size, snapshot.mtime_ns):
                    # The watcher already replaced this row with a new file's
                    # pending state; the response still belongs to the old file.
                    return "stale"
                if photo.revision != snapshot.revision:
                    # A user edit made during the request owns both the result and filename.
                    self._mark(photo, "processed")
                    self._publish()
                    return "edited"
                try:
                    fingerprint = self._fingerprint(snapshot.path)
                except OSError:
                    return "stale"
                if fingerprint != (snapshot.size, snapshot.mtime_ns):
                    self._poll()
                    return "stale"
                if cancel.is_set():
                    raise Cancelled()
                if not reuse:
                    photo.analysis = analysis
                    photo.proposed_name = filename_from_analysis(analysis or {}, photo.path.suffix)
                    photo.status, photo.error, photo.model = "분석 완료", "", config[1]
                    cache.put(photo)
                self._consecutive_failures = 0
                if automatic:
                    assert self._root is not None
                    # Analysis is cached before renaming. A restart can finish this without a second API call.
                    try:
                        pairs = apply_names(self._root, [photo], cache)
                    except Exception as exc:
                        photo.status, photo.error = "파일명 오류", str(exc)
                        cache.put(photo)
                        self._mark(photo, "error")
                        self._publish()
                        self._emit("error", f"자동 파일명 변경 실패: {exc}")
                        return "rename_error"
                    self._remap(pairs)
                    self._mark(photo, "processed")
                else:
                    self._mark(photo, "processed")
                self._publish()
                return "success"
        except Cancelled:
            with self._lock:
                photo = self._photos.get(identifier)
                if (photo is not None and photo.revision == snapshot.revision
                        and (photo.size, photo.mtime_ns) == (snapshot.size, snapshot.mtime_ns)):
                    photo.status = snapshot.status if snapshot.status != "분석 중" else "대기"
                    photo.error = snapshot.error
                    cache.put(photo)
                    self._publish()
            return "cancelled"
        except Exception as exc:
            with self._lock:
                photo = self._photos.get(identifier)
                if photo is None or (photo.size, photo.mtime_ns) != (snapshot.size, snapshot.mtime_ns):
                    return "stale"
                if config != self._config:
                    # A request already sent keeps its original credentials.
                    # Its failure must not pause work configured with a new key
                    # or model, or overwrite an edit made while it was running.
                    if photo.revision != snapshot.revision:
                        return "edited"
                    try:
                        fingerprint = self._fingerprint(snapshot.path)
                    except OSError:
                        return "stale"
                    if fingerprint != (snapshot.size, snapshot.mtime_ns):
                        self._poll()
                        return "stale"
                    photo.status = snapshot.status if snapshot.status != "분석 중" else "대기"
                    photo.error = snapshot.error
                    cache.put(photo)
                    self._mark(photo, "pending")
                    self._publish()
                    self._emit("status", "API 설정이 변경되어 이전 요청의 오류를 제외하고 사진을 대기 상태로 돌렸습니다.")
                    return "reconfigured"
                if photo.revision == snapshot.revision:
                    photo.status, photo.error = "오류", str(exc)
                    cache.put(photo)
                    self._mark(photo, "error")
                    self._publish()
                self._consecutive_failures += 1
                # Only the current request's explicit model retirement belongs
                # in persistent UI settings. Generic 410s and earlier API
                # configurations must not exclude a model for future runs.
                if (isinstance(exc, APIError) and config[1]
                        and getattr(exc, "model_retired", None) == config[1]):
                    self._emit("model_retired", (config[0], config[1]))
                fatal = bool(getattr(exc, "fatal", False))
                if fatal or self._consecutive_failures >= 3:
                    self._pause_for_failure(str(exc) if fatal else "3장 연속 오류로 분석을 중지했습니다. " + str(exc))
                    return "halt"
                self._emit("status", f"분석 오류: {snapshot.path.name} — {exc}")
            return "error"

    def _run_manual(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            identifiers = [str(p.path) for p in self._ordered() if self._needs_analysis(p)]
        halted = False
        for identifier in identifiers:
            if self._cancel.is_set() or self._closed:
                break
            result = self._process(identifier, automatic=False)
            if result == "reconfigured" and not self._cancel.is_set() and not self._closed:
                # Retry this photo once; repeated settings edits must not keep
                # the rest of a manual batch waiting indefinitely.
                result = self._process(identifier, automatic=False)
            if result in {"halt", "cancelled"}:
                halted = result == "halt"
                break
        with self._lock:
            if self._cache is not None:
                changed = unify_runs(sorted(self._photos.values(), key=lambda p: (
                    natural_key(str(p.path.parent)), natural_key(p.path.name))))
                if changed:
                    self._cache.put_many(changed)
                    self._publish()
            if halted:
                message = "오류로 분석을 중지했습니다. API 설정을 확인한 뒤 다시 시작하세요."
            elif self._cancel.is_set():
                message = "분석을 중지했습니다."
            else:
                message = "남은 사진 분석을 마쳤습니다."
            self._emit("status", message)

    def _run_watch(self) -> None:
        """Keep arrivals visible while the worker waits for an API response.

        Rename transactions keep the same lock through staging and commit, so
        the scanner never observes their temporary missing or partial state.
        """
        while not self._watch_stop.wait(self._interval):
            with self._lock:
                if self._closed:
                    return
                if self._root is None or time.monotonic() - self._last_poll < self._interval:
                    continue
                try:
                    self._poll()
                except Exception as exc:
                    self._pause_for_failure(f"폴더 감시 오류: {exc}")
            # The worker remains the only consumer of queued analysis tasks.
            self._wake.set()

    def _run(self) -> None:
        while True:
            self._wake.wait(self._interval)
            self._wake.clear()
            with self._lock:
                if self._closed:
                    return
                manual = self._manual_requested
                if manual:
                    self._manual_requested = False
                elif self._busy:
                    continue
                else:
                    try:
                        identifier = self._ready_auto_photo() if self._auto else None
                    except Exception as exc:
                        self._pause_for_failure(f"폴더 감시 오류: {exc}")
                        continue
                    if identifier is None:
                        continue
                    self._busy, self._operation = True, "auto"
                    self._cancel = threading.Event()
                    self._emit("busy", True)
            try:
                if manual:
                    self._run_manual()
                else:
                    self._process(identifier, automatic=True)
            except Exception as exc:
                with self._lock:
                    self._pause_for_failure(f"분석 작업 오류: {exc}")
            finally:
                with self._lock:
                    self._busy, self._operation = False, ""
                    self._emit("busy", False)
                    if self._auto:
                        self._emit("status", "새 사진을 감시하고 있습니다.")
                self._wake.set()

    def update_photo(self, path: str, field: str, value: str) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            photo = self._photos.get(str(Path(path).resolve()))
            if photo is None:
                return
            try:
                if field == "proposed_name":
                    name = sanitize_filename(value.strip(), photo.path.suffix)
                    photo.proposed_name = name
                elif field in {"gender", "clothing", "objects"}:
                    photo.analysis = copy.deepcopy(photo.analysis or {"gender": "불명", "clothing": [], "objects": []})
                    if field == "gender":
                        photo.analysis[field] = value.strip() or "불명"
                    else:
                        photo.analysis[field] = [part.strip() for part in re.split(r"[,，\n;]+", value)
                                                 if part.strip() and part.strip() not in {"없음", "-"}]
                    photo.proposed_name = filename_from_analysis(photo.analysis, photo.path.suffix)
                else:
                    raise ValueError("수정할 수 없는 항목입니다.")
                photo.manual, photo.confirmed = True, False
                photo.status, photo.error = "수동 수정", ""
                photo.revision += 1
                assert self._cache is not None
                self._cache.put(photo)
                self._mark(photo, "processed")
                self._publish()
            except Exception as exc:
                self._emit("error", str(exc))

    def unify_selected(self, paths: list[str], reference: str) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            base = self._photos.get(str(Path(reference).resolve()))
            if base is None or not base.analysis:
                self._emit("error", "분석된 기준 사진을 선택하세요.")
                return
            selected = [self._photos[p] for p in dict.fromkeys(str(Path(p).resolve()) for p in paths)
                        if p in self._photos and self._photos[p].analysis
                        and not self._photos[p].error and self._photos[p].status != "분석 중"]
            if len(selected) < 2:
                self._emit("error", "분석을 완료한 사진을 두 장 이상 선택하세요.")
                return
            changed: list[Photo] = []
            reference_analysis = copy.deepcopy(base.analysis)
            for photo in selected:
                photo.analysis = copy.deepcopy(reference_analysis)
                photo.proposed_name = filename_from_analysis(photo.analysis, photo.path.suffix)
                photo.manual, photo.confirmed = True, False
                photo.status, photo.error = "수동 수정", ""
                photo.revision += 1
                self._known[self._key(photo.path)] = self._record(photo, "processed")
                changed.append(photo)
            if changed:
                assert self._cache is not None
                self._cache.put_many(changed)
                self._save_manifest()
                self._publish()

    def select_through(self, path: str) -> list[str]:
        with self._lock:
            photo = self._photos.get(str(Path(path).resolve()))
            return [str(p.path) for p in select_through(self._ordered(), photo)] if photo else []

    def _remap(self, pairs: list[tuple[Path, Path]]) -> None:
        # Remove all sources first: one operation may swap two existing names.
        for before, _ in pairs:
            old = self._key(Path(before))
            self._known.pop(old, None)
            self._stable.pop(old, None)
        for before, after in pairs:
            new = self._key(Path(after))
            try:
                fingerprint = self._fingerprint(Path(after))
            except OSError:
                continue
            self._known[new] = {"size": fingerprint[0], "mtime_ns": fingerprint[1],
                                "state": "processed", "present": True}
            self._stable[new] = (fingerprint, self._stable_scans)
        self._photos = {str(p.path): p for p in self._photos.values() if p.path.exists()}
        self._save_manifest()

    def _rename_action(self, paths: list[str] | None) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            if self._busy or self._closed:
                self._emit("error", "분석이 끝난 후 파일명을 적용하거나 되돌리세요.")
                return
            if self._root is None or self._cache is None:
                self._emit("error", "먼저 사진 폴더를 선택하세요.")
                return
            self._busy = True
            self._emit("busy", True)
            try:
                if paths is None:
                    pairs = undo_last(self._root, self._cache)
                    # The journal/cache restores pre-rename metadata, not the current snapshots.
                    # Remove every current name first so swapped filenames cannot erase restored rows.
                    for before, _ in pairs:
                        self._photos.pop(str(before), None)
                    for _, after in pairs:
                        photo = self._cache.get(after) or Photo.from_path(after)
                        self._photos[str(photo.path)] = photo
                    self._remap(pairs)
                    self._emit("status", f"사진 {len(pairs):,}장의 마지막 변경을 되돌렸습니다.")
                else:
                    photos = [self._photos[p] for p in dict.fromkeys(str(Path(p).resolve()) for p in paths)
                              if p in self._photos and not self._photos[p].confirmed
                              and (not is_gender_prefixed_filename(self._photos[p].path.name)
                                   or self._photos[p].manual)
                              and self._photos[p].proposed_name]
                    pairs = apply_names(self._root, photos, self._cache)
                    self._remap(pairs)
                    for photo in photos:
                        if photo.confirmed:
                            self._known[self._key(photo.path)] = self._record(photo, "processed")
                    self._save_manifest()
                    self._emit("status", f"사진 {len(photos):,}장의 파일명을 적용했습니다.")
                self._publish()
            except Exception as exc:
                self._emit("error", f"파일명 변경 오류: {exc}")
            finally:
                self._busy = False
                self._emit("busy", False)
        self._wake.set()

    def apply_selected(self, paths: list[str]) -> None:
        self._rename_action(paths)

    def undo(self) -> None:
        self._rename_action(None)

    def shutdown(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            if self._closed:
                return
            self._closed = True
            self._auto = False
            self._cancel.set()
            self._save_manifest()
        self._wake.set()
        self._watch_stop.set()
        for thread in (self._watcher, self._worker):
            if threading.current_thread() is thread:
                continue
            thread.join(timeout=5.0)
            if thread.is_alive():
                message = ("폴더 감시 작업의 종료를 기다리고 있습니다." if thread is self._watcher
                           else "분석 작업의 종료를 기다리고 있습니다. 네트워크 요청은 취소되었습니다.")
                self._emit("error", message)

===== END FILE {"path": "frametag/engine.py"} =====

"""Tk interface; worker callbacks are delivered through the main-thread queue."""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import OrderedDict
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
import os
from pathlib import Path
import queue
import re
import threading
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import filedialog, messagebox, ttk

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageOps, ImageTk

from . import __version__
from .api import (APIError, Cancelled, ModelDiscovery,
                  _safe_detail, diagnose_connection, discover_photo_models, list_models)
from .domain import is_gender_prefixed_filename, select_through
from .model_catalog import DEFAULT_MODELS, RETIRED_NVIDIA_MODELS, model_id, model_label, restore_model
from .engine import Engine
from .models import Photo
from .storage import KeyStore, SettingsStore


class FrameTagApp(tk.Tk):
    def __init__(self, background=False, settings_store=None, key_store=None,
                 engine_factory=Engine):
        super().__init__()
        self.title(f"FrameTag {__version__}")
        self.minsize(1050, 680)
        self.configure(background="#edf5ef")
        self.settings_store = settings_store or SettingsStore()
        self.key_store = key_store or KeyStore()
        self.events = queue.Queue()
        self.engine = engine_factory(lambda name, data: self.events.put((name, data)))
        self._engine_pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1, thread_name_prefix="engine-control")
        self.photos: list[Photo] = []
        self.by_id: dict[str, Photo] = {}
        self.reference = ""
        self._busy = False
        self._loading = False
        self._auto_pending = False
        self._diagnosing = False
        self._diagnostic_cancel = threading.Event()
        self._api_revision = 0
        self._models_pending = False
        self._model_cancel = threading.Event()
        self._resume_auto_after_models = False
        self._resume_model_after_models = ""
        self._verified_models = {}
        self._loaded_folder = None
        self._closed = False
        self._tray = None
        self._poll_id = None
        self._save_id = None
        self._search_id = None
        self._preview_id = None
        self._preview_token = 0
        self._preview_image = None
        self._preview_tk = None
        self._preview_cache = OrderedDict()
        self._preview_pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1, thread_name_prefix="preview")
        self._zoom = None
        self._editor = None
        self._provider_keys = {}
        self._saved_keys = {}
        self._key_dirty = False
        self._loading_key = False
        self._key_save_id = None
        try:
            saved = self.settings_store.load()
        except Exception:
            saved = {}
        self._saved = saved
        self._models = saved.get("models", {}) if isinstance(saved.get("models"), dict) else {}
        retired = saved.get("retired_nvidia_models", [])
        self._retired_nvidia = set(RETIRED_NVIDIA_MODELS)
        if isinstance(retired, list):
            self._retired_nvidia.update(item for item in retired if isinstance(item, str)
                and re.fullmatch(r"[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+/[a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+", item))
        provider = saved.get("provider", "NVIDIA")
        if provider not in ("NVIDIA", "OpenAI"):
            provider = "NVIDIA"
        self.folder = tk.StringVar(value=saved.get("folder", ""))
        self.provider = tk.StringVar(value=provider)
        self.model = tk.StringVar(value=restore_model(provider, self._models.get(provider, "")))
        if provider == "NVIDIA" and self.model.get() in self._retired_nvidia:
            self.model.set("")
        self.model_choice = tk.StringVar(value=model_label(provider, self.model.get()))
        self.api_key = tk.StringVar()
        self.recursive = tk.BooleanVar(value=bool(saved.get("recursive", False)))
        self.auto_watch = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)
        self.close_to_tray = tk.BooleanVar(value=bool(saved.get("close_to_tray", True)))
        self.show_completed = tk.BooleanVar(value=bool(saved.get("show_completed", False)))
        self.search = tk.StringVar()
        self.status = tk.StringVar(value="폴더와 API 키를 선택하세요. 설정은 다음 실행에도 유지됩니다.")
        self.loading_text = tk.StringVar()
        self.count = tk.StringVar(value="0 / 0")
        self.preview_label = tk.StringVar(value="사진을 선택하면 미리보기가 표시됩니다.")
        self.zoom_label = tk.StringVar(value="맞춤")
        self._apply_style()
        self._build()
        try:
            self.geometry(saved.get("geometry", "1450x900"))
        except tk.TclError:
            self.geometry("1450x900")
        self.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self._on_close)
        self.bind("<Control-f>", lambda event: self.search_entry.focus_set())
        self.search.trace_add("write", self._search_changed)
        self.api_key.trace_add("write", self._key_changed)
        self.folder.trace_add("write", self._folder_changed)
        self.model.trace_add("write", self._model_changed)
        self.model_choice.trace_add("write", self._model_choice_changed)
        self._active_provider = provider
        self._load_key(provider)
        self._setup_tray()
        self._poll_id = self.after(80, self._poll)
        self.after(120, self._restore_sash)
        self.after(200, lambda: self._startup(bool(saved.get("auto_watch", False)), background))

    @staticmethod
    def _defaults(provider):
        defaults = DEFAULT_MODELS.get(provider, [])
        if isinstance(defaults, str):
            return [defaults]
        return list(defaults)

    def _model_changed(self, *args):
        self._api_revision += 1
        self._pause_for_api_change()
        label = model_label(self.provider.get(), self.model.get())
        if self.model_choice.get() != label:
            self.model_choice.set(label)
        self._schedule_save()

    def _pause_for_api_change(self):
        if self._models_pending:
            self._model_cancel.set()
            self._resume_auto_after_models = False
        if self._busy or self.auto_watch.get():
            self.auto_watch.set(False)
            self._dispatch_engine(self.engine.stop_analysis)
            self.status.set("API 설정이 바뀌어 분석을 중지했습니다. 새 설정의 모델을 확인한 뒤 다시 시작하세요.")

    def _model_choice_changed(self, *args):
        identifier = model_id(self.provider.get(), self.model_choice.get())
        if self.model.get() != identifier:
            self.model.set(identifier)

    def _dispatch_engine(self, action, *args, done=None):
        """Keep filesystem/engine locks off Tk's event thread, in FIFO order."""
        if self._closed:
            return
        def run():
            try:
                if not self._closed:
                    action(*args)
            except Exception as exc:
                self.events.put(("error", str(exc)))
            finally:
                if done:
                    self.events.put((done, None))
        self._engine_pool.submit(run)

    def _update_loading_indicator(self):
        active = self._loading or self._auto_pending or self._diagnosing or self._models_pending
        self.loading_text.set("로딩 중 · 사진 목록과 저장된 결과를 불러오고 있습니다." if self._loading else
                              "처리 중 · 자동 감시 설정을 적용하고 있습니다." if self._auto_pending else
                              "모델 확인 중 · 서버 응답을 기다리고 있습니다." if self._models_pending else
                              "API 연결 진단 중 · 서버 응답을 기다리고 있습니다." if self._diagnosing else "")
        if active and str(self.progress["mode"]) != "indeterminate":
            self.progress.configure(mode="indeterminate")
            self.progress.start(40)
        elif not active and str(self.progress["mode"]) == "indeterminate":
            self.progress.stop()
            self.progress.configure(mode="determinate")
            self.progress["value"] = 100 * sum(bool(p.analysis) for p in self.photos) / len(self.photos) if self.photos else 0

    def _apply_style(self):
        self.option_add("*Font", ("Segoe UI", 10))
        style = ttk.Style(self)
        style.theme_use("clam")
        style.configure("TFrame", background="#edf5ef")
        style.configure("Header.TFrame", background="#e2f0e6")
        style.configure("TLabel", background="#edf5ef", foreground="#254739")
        style.configure("Header.TLabel", background="#e2f0e6")
        style.configure("TCheckbutton", background="#edf5ef", foreground="#254739")
        style.configure("TButton", padding=(9, 7), background="#ffffff", foreground="#315343")
        style.map("TButton", background=[("active", "#d4e8da")])
        style.configure("Accent.TButton", background="#2f6c51", foreground="white")
        style.map("Accent.TButton", background=[("active", "#25583f")])
        style.configure("Treeview", background="white", fieldbackground="white", rowheight=28)
        style.configure("Treeview.Heading", background="#e2eee5", foreground="#254739", padding=8)
        style.map("Treeview", background=[("selected", "#b9d9c5")], foreground=[("selected", "#153526")])

    def _build(self):
        header = ttk.Frame(self, style="Header.TFrame", padding=10)
        header.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=(10, 5))
        header.columnconfigure(1, weight=1)
        ttk.Label(header, text="사진 폴더", style="Header.TLabel").grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="w", padx=(0, 8))
        self.folder_entry = ttk.Entry(header, textvariable=self.folder)
        self.folder_entry.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ew", padx=(0, 8), ipady=5)
        self.folder_entry.bind("<Return>", lambda event: self._load_folder())
        self.browse_button = ttk.Button(header, text="폴더 선택", command=self._browse)
        self.browse_button.grid(row=0, column=2, padx=(0, 8))
        self.recursive_check = ttk.Checkbutton(header, text="하위 폴더 포함", variable=self.recursive,
                                               command=self._recursive_changed)
        self.recursive_check.grid(row=0, column=3, sticky="w")
        self.diagnostic_button = ttk.Button(header, text="API 연결 진단", command=self._diagnose_api)
        self.diagnostic_button.grid(row=0, column=4, padx=(5, 0))
        self.api_label = ttk.Label(header, text="NVIDIA API 키", style="Header.TLabel")
        self.api_label.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="w", padx=(0, 8), pady=(8, 0))
        self.key_entry = ttk.Entry(header, textvariable=self.api_key, show="●")
        self.key_entry.grid(row=1, column=1, sticky="ew", padx=(0, 8), pady=(8, 0), ipady=5)
        self.key_entry.bind("<FocusOut>", lambda event: self._save_key())
        self.provider_combo = ttk.Combobox(header, textvariable=self.provider, values=("NVIDIA", "OpenAI"),
                                           state="readonly", width=10)
        self.provider_combo.grid(row=1, column=2, pady=(8, 0), padx=(0, 8))
        self.provider_combo.bind("<<ComboboxSelected>>", self._provider_changed)
        self.model_combo = ttk.Combobox(header, textvariable=self.model_choice, width=38,
                                       values=[model_label(self.provider.get(), identifier)

        self.model_combo.grid(row=1, column=3, pady=(8, 0), padx=(0, 5), sticky="ew")

        self.models_button.grid(row=1, column=4, pady=(8, 0))
        ttk.Button(header, text="설정 저장", command=lambda: self._save_settings(explicit=True)).grid(
            row=1, column=5, padx=(5, 0), pady=(8, 0))

        toolbar = ttk.Frame(self)
        toolbar.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=4)
        self.load_button = ttk.Button(toolbar, text="목록 불러오기", command=self._load_folder)
        self.load_button.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 5))
        self.analyze_button = ttk.Button(toolbar, text="남은 사진 분석", style="Accent.TButton", command=self._analyze)
        self.analyze_button.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 5))
        ttk.Button(toolbar, text="선택 셀 수정", command=self._edit_selected).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 5))
        ttk.Button(toolbar, text="선택 사진 기준 통일", command=self._unify).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 5))
        ttk.Button(toolbar, text="현재 사진까지 선택", command=self._select_through).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 5))
        self.apply_button = ttk.Button(toolbar, text="선택 파일명 적용", command=self._apply)
        self.apply_button.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 5))
        self.undo_button = ttk.Button(toolbar, text="마지막 변경 되돌리기", command=self._undo)
        self.undo_button.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 5))
        self.stop_button = ttk.Button(toolbar, text="분석 중지", command=self._stop_analysis, state="disabled")
        self.stop_button.pack(side="right")
        monitor = ttk.Frame(self)
        monitor.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=4)
        self.auto_check = ttk.Checkbutton(monitor, text="새 사진 자동 분석·이름 변경", variable=self.auto_watch,
                                          command=self._toggle_auto)
        self.auto_check.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 15))
        ttk.Checkbutton(monitor, text="창 닫을 때 트레이로 숨기기", variable=self.close_to_tray,
                        command=self._schedule_save).pack(side="left")
        ttk.Checkbutton(monitor, text="이름 변경 완료 사진도 표시", variable=self.show_completed,
                        command=self._completed_filter_changed).pack(side="left", padx=(15, 0))
        ttk.Button(monitor, text="백그라운드로 숨기기", command=self._hide).pack(side="right", padx=(5, 0))
        ttk.Button(monitor, text="완전히 종료", command=self._exit).pack(side="right")

        search_bar = ttk.Frame(self)
        search_bar.pack(fill="x", padx=10, pady=(5, 7))
        ttk.Label(search_bar, text="목록 검색").pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))

        self.pane = ttk.Panedwindow(self, orient="horizontal")
        self.pane.pack(fill="both", expand=True, padx=10)
        listing = ttk.Frame(self.pane)
        self.pane.add(listing, weight=3)
        columns = ("status", "name", "gender", "clothing", "objects", "proposed_name")
        self.tree = ttk.Treeview(listing, columns=columns, show="headings", selectmode="extended")
        for column, title, width in zip(columns,
                ("상태", "현재 파일명", "성별", "의류", "정물", "새 파일명"),
                (95, 180, 70, 205, 210, 260)):
            self.tree.heading(column, text=title)
            self.tree.column(column, width=width, minwidth=55, stretch=True)

        hscroll = ttk.Scrollbar(listing, orient="horizontal", command=self.tree.xview)
        self.tree.configure(yscrollcommand=vscroll.set, xscrollcommand=hscroll.set)
        self.tree.grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="nsew")
        vscroll.grid(row=0, column=1, sticky="ns")
        hscroll.grid(row=1, column=0, sticky="ew")
        listing.rowconfigure(0, weight=1)
        listing.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        self.tree.tag_configure("odd", background="#f3f8f5")
        self.tree.tag_configure("reference", foreground="#227346")
        self.tree.tag_configure("error", foreground="#a03737")
        self.tree.bind("<<TreeviewSelect>>", self._selection_changed)
        self.tree.bind("<Double-1>", self._edit_cell)
        self.tree.bind("<Button-3>", self._context_menu)
        self.tree.bind("<Return>", lambda event: self._edit_selected())
        preview = ttk.Frame(self.pane, padding=8)
        self.pane.add(preview, weight=2)
        preview_top = ttk.Frame(preview)
        preview_top.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 8))
        ttk.Label(preview_top, text="미리보기", font=("Segoe UI", 12, "bold")).pack(side="left")

        for text, command in (("+", lambda: self._zoom_by(1.25)),
                              ("맞춤", self._fit), ("−", lambda: self._zoom_by(0.8))):
            ttk.Button(preview_top, text=text, width=5, command=command).pack(side="right", padx=2)
        canvas_frame = ttk.Frame(preview)
        canvas_frame.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
