        for identifier in NVIDIA_DISCOVERABLE:
            self.assertEqual(model_id("NVIDIA", model_label("NVIDIA", identifier)), identifier)
        self.assertEqual(restore_model("OpenAI", "custom-vision-id"), "custom-vision-id")

    def test_confirmed_builtin_retired_model_never_reads_or_uploads_a_photo(self):
        model = "qwen/qwen3.5-122b-a10b"
        analyzer = Analyzer("NVIDIA", model, "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(
            lambda request: self.fail("A confirmed retired model must not send a request")))
        with patch("frametag.api._image_url", side_effect=AssertionError("User photo read")):
            with self.assertRaises(APIError) as caught:
                analyzer.analyze(Path("missing-user-photo.jpg"), threading.Event())
        self.assertTrue(caught.exception.fatal)
        self.assertEqual(caught.exception.model_retired, model)
        self.assertIsNone(caught.exception.status_code)

    def test_error_retains_server_reason_and_request_id_without_echoed_secrets(self):
        key = "nvapi-dummy-private-value"
        other_key = "sk-other-dummy-secret"
        uri = "data:image/jpeg;base64," + "A" * 200
        def handler(request):
            return httpx.Response(410, json={"error": {"message":
                f"Account route disabled. key={key}; other={other_key}; image={uri}"},
                "request": {"Authorization": key, "messages": "private photo data"}},
                headers={"x-request-id": "trace-test"})
        with self.assertRaises(APIError) as caught:
            Analyzer("NVIDIA", "test-model", key, transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)).analyze(
                self.photo, threading.Event())
        message = str(caught.exception)
        self.assertTrue(caught.exception.fatal)
        self.assertEqual(caught.exception.status_code, 410)
        self.assertIn("Account route disabled", message)
        self.assertIn("POST https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1/chat/completions", message)
        self.assertIn("trace-test", message)
        self.assertIn("단정할 수 없습니다", message)
        for private in (key, other_key, uri, "private photo data", "A" * 100):
            self.assertNotIn(private, message)

    def test_plain_text_and_html_errors_are_bounded_and_redacted(self):
        key = "dummy-value-without-standard-prefix"
        for content in (f"Gateway gone Bearer {key} data:image/jpeg;base64,AAAA " + "x" * 3000,
                        f"<html><script>secret script</script><body>Gateway gone {key}</body></html>"):
            with self.subTest(content=content[:30]):
                def handler(request):
                    return httpx.Response(410, text=content)
                with self.assertRaises(APIError) as caught:
                    Analyzer("NVIDIA", "test-model", key, transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)).analyze(
                        self.photo, threading.Event())
                message = str(caught.exception)
                self.assertIn("Gateway gone", message)
                self.assertNotIn(key, message)
                self.assertNotIn("secret script", message)
                self.assertNotIn("data:image", message)
                self.assertLess(len(message), 1500)

    def test_wrapped_base64_error_is_hidden_in_copyable_diagnostic(self):
        wrapped = "\n".join(["A" * 76, "B" * 76, "C" * 76])
        def handler(request):
            return httpx.Response(410, json={"error": {
                "message": 'Image rejected: "data:image/jpeg;base64,\n' + wrapped + '" route disabled'}})
        report = diagnose_connection("NVIDIA", "test-model", "test-placeholder",
                                     transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
        self.assertIn("Image rejected", report)
        self.assertIn("route disabled", report)
        self.assertIn("이미지 데이터 숨김", report)
        for chunk in ("A" * 76, "B" * 76, "C" * 76, "data:image"):
            self.assertNotIn(chunk, report)

    def test_diagnostic_compares_catalog_and_inference_without_photo_data(self):
        key = "nvapi-dummy-diagnostic-key"
        model = "qwen/qwen3.5-397b-a17b"
        requests = []
        def handler(request):
            requests.append(request)
            self.assertEqual(request.headers["Authorization"], "Bearer " + key)
            if request.method == "GET":
                return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model}]})
            payload = json.loads(request.content)
            self.assertEqual(payload["model"], model)
            self.assertEqual(payload["max_tokens"], 32)
            self.assertEqual(payload["chat_template_kwargs"], {"enable_thinking": False})
            self.assertIsInstance(payload["messages"][0]["content"], str)
            self.assertNotIn("image_url", request.content.decode())
            self.assertNotIn(str(self.photo), request.content.decode())
            return httpx.Response(410, json={"error": {"message": "Inference route not available"}})
        report = diagnose_connection("NVIDIA", model, key, transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
        self.assertEqual([r.method for r in requests], ["GET", "POST"])
        self.assertIn("HTTP 200", report)
        self.assertIn("목록에 있습니다", report)
        self.assertIn("410", report)
        self.assertIn("Inference route not available", report)
        self.assertNotIn(key, report)

    def test_diagnostic_checks_inference_even_when_catalog_is_gone(self):
        for inference_status in (200, 410):
            with self.subTest(inference_status=inference_status):
                calls = []
                def handler(request):
                    calls.append(request.method)
                    if request.method == "GET":
                        return httpx.Response(410, text="Catalog unavailable")
                    return reply(status=inference_status)
                report = diagnose_connection("NVIDIA", "custom-model", "test-placeholder",
                                             transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
                self.assertEqual(calls, ["GET", "POST"])
                self.assertIn("Catalog unavailable", report)
                if inference_status == 200:
                    self.assertIn("텍스트 응답을 받았습니다", report)
                    self.assertIn("별도 확인", report)
                else:
                    self.assertEqual(report.count("(410)"), 2)

    def test_diagnostic_does_not_infer_after_auth_or_quota_failure(self):
        for status in (401, 402, 403, 429):
            with self.subTest(status=status):

    def test_diagnostic_handles_malformed_catalog_but_tests_inference(self):

            return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": None}) if request.method == "GET" else reply()
        report = diagnose_connection("NVIDIA", "test-model", "test-placeholder",
                                     transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
        self.assertIn("모델 목록 형식", report)
        self.assertIn("텍스트 응답을 받았습니다", report)

    def test_cancellation_interrupts_diagnostic_and_skips_second_route(self):
        entered = threading.Event()
        cancel = threading.Event()
        calls = []
        async def handler(request):
            calls.append(request.method)
            entered.set()
            await asyncio.sleep(10)
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": []})
        def cancel_soon():
            entered.wait(1)
            cancel.set()
        thread = threading.Thread(target=cancel_soon, daemon=True)
        thread.start()
        start = time.monotonic()
        with self.assertRaises(Cancelled):
            diagnose_connection("NVIDIA", "test-model", "test-placeholder", cancel,
                                transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
        thread.join(1)
        self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - start, 1)
        self.assertEqual(calls, ["GET"])

    def test_explicit_model_retirement_requires_matching_requested_model(self):
        model = "vendor/test-vision"
        descriptions = [
            (f"The model '{model}' has reached its end of life on 2026-07-20 and is no longer available.", model),
            (f"The model '{model}' is no longer available.", model),
            ("The model 'different-model' has reached its end of life and is no longer available.", ""),
            (f"The model '{model}' is active. The model 'another-model' has reached its end of life.", ""),
            (f"Route gone for {model}; contact the account owner.", ""),
        ]
        for description, retired in descriptions:
            with self.subTest(description=description):
                def handler(request):
                    return httpx.Response(410, json={"error": {"message": description}})
                with self.assertRaises(APIError) as caught:
                    Analyzer("NVIDIA", model, "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)).analyze(
                        self.photo, threading.Event())
                self.assertEqual(caught.exception.model_retired, retired)
                self.assertIn(description, str(caught.exception))

    def test_discovery_offers_only_actually_verified_images_and_never_opens_user_files(self):
        available = ["meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct", "vendor/new-vision-v1",
                     "nvidia/nemotron-nano-12b-v2-vl"]
        requests, progress = [], []
        def handler(request):
            requests.append(request)
            if request.method == "GET":
                return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": name} for name in available]})
            payload = json.loads(request.content)
            self.assertEqual(payload["max_tokens"], 128)
            self.assertNotIn(str(self.photo), request.content.decode())
            self.assertNotIn("photo.png", request.content.decode())
            return challenge_reply(request)
        with patch("frametag.api._image_url", side_effect=AssertionError("User image read")):
            result = discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler),
                                           progress=lambda *values: progress.append(values))
        self.assertEqual(result.models, ["nvidia/nemotron-nano-12b-v2-vl", "vendor/new-vision-v1"])
        self.assertEqual(result.catalog, sorted(available))
        self.assertFalse(result.retired)
        self.assertEqual(progress, [(1, 2, result.models[0]), (2, 2, result.models[1])])
        self.assertEqual(len(requests), 3)

    def test_successful_text_response_without_correct_image_colors_is_not_verified(self):
        model = "vendor/new-vision-v1"
        def handler(request):
            if request.method == "GET":
                return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model}]})
            return reply('{"left":"blue","right":"red"}')
        with patch("frametag.api.secrets.choice", side_effect=["red", "green"]):
            result = discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
        self.assertEqual(result.models, [])
        self.assertEqual(len(result.notes), 1)
        self.assertIn("색상과 위치", result.notes[0])

    def test_discovery_skips_confirmed_and_saved_retired_ids_without_probing(self):
        model = "vendor/remaining-vision"
        retired = "qwen/qwen3.5-122b-a10b"
        excluded = "vendor/saved-retired-vision"
        probed = []
        def handler(request):
            if request.method == "GET":
                return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": identifier}
                    for identifier in (model, retired, excluded)]})
            probed.append(json.loads(request.content)["model"])
            return challenge_reply(request)
        result = discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", preferred=retired, excluded=(excluded,),
                                       transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
        self.assertEqual(result.models, [model])
        self.assertEqual(probed, [model])

    def test_discovery_handles_individual_retirement_and_unsupported_images(self):
        models = ["vendor/a-vision", "vendor/b-vision", "vendor/c-vision", "vendor/d-vision"]
        requests = []
        def handler(request):
            requests.append(request)
            if request.method == "GET":
                return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model} for model in models]})
            model = json.loads(request.content)["model"]
            if model == models[0]:
                return httpx.Response(410, json={"error": {"message":
                    f"The model '{model}' has reached its end of life and is no longer available."}})
            if model == models[1]:
                return httpx.Response(400, json={"error": "Image input is not supported"})
            if model == models[2]:
                return httpx.Response(404, json={"error": "Model not found"})
            return challenge_reply(request)
        result = discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
        self.assertEqual(result.models, [models[3]])
        self.assertEqual(result.retired, [models[0]])
        self.assertEqual(len(result.notes), 3)
        self.assertEqual(len(requests), 5)

    def test_auth_and_quota_abort_discovery_without_trying_other_models(self):
        models = ["vendor/a-vision", "vendor/b-vision"]
        for status in (401, 402, 403, 429):
            with self.subTest(status=status):
                calls = []
                def handler(request):
                    calls.append(request.method)
                    if request.method == "GET":
                        return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model} for model in models]})
                    return httpx.Response(status, json={"error": "Account route unavailable"})
                with self.assertRaises(APIError) as caught:
                    discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
                self.assertEqual(calls, ["GET", "POST"])
                self.assertEqual(caught.exception.status_code, status)
                self.assertEqual(caught.exception.model_retired, "")
                self.assertEqual(caught.exception.retired_models, [])

    def test_later_global_discovery_error_preserves_previously_confirmed_retirement(self):
        models = ["vendor/a-vision", "vendor/b-vision"]
        def handler(request):
            if request.method == "GET":
                return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model} for model in models]})
            model = json.loads(request.content)["model"]
            if model == models[0]:
                return httpx.Response(410, json={"error": f"The model '{model}' is no longer available."})
            return httpx.Response(403, json={"error": "Account restricted"})
        with self.assertRaises(APIError) as caught:
            discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
        self.assertEqual(caught.exception.retired_models, [models[0]])

    def test_discovery_limit_and_preferred_catalog_model(self):
        models = [f"vendor/test-{index:02d}-vision" for index in range(12)]
        preferred = "vendor/custom-vision"
        probed = []
        def handler(request):
            if request.method == "GET":
                return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model} for model in [*models, preferred]]})
            probed.append(json.loads(request.content)["model"])
            return reply("not a color response")
        result = discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", preferred=preferred,
                                       transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
        self.assertEqual(probed, [preferred, *models[:7]])
        self.assertEqual(result.models, [])
        self.assertEqual(len(result.catalog), 13)

    def test_discovery_stops_after_three_verified_models(self):
        models = [f"vendor/test-{index:02d}-vision" for index in range(8)]
        probed = []
        def handler(request):
            if request.method == "GET":
                return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model} for model in models]})
            probed.append(json.loads(request.content)["model"])
            return challenge_reply(request)
        result = discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
        self.assertEqual(result.models, models[:3])
        self.assertEqual(probed, models[:3])

    def test_discovery_network_failure_is_not_retried(self):
        calls = []
        def handler(request):
            calls.append(request.method)
            if request.method == "GET":
                return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": "vendor/test-vision"}]})
            raise httpx.ConnectError("unavailable", request=request)
        with self.assertRaises(APIError):
            discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
        self.assertEqual(calls, ["GET", "POST"])

    def test_model_http_rejections_preserve_verified_models_and_continue(self):
        models = ["vendor/a-vision", "vendor/b-vision", "vendor/c-vision"]
        for status in (400, 404, 410, 422, 500, 503):
            with self.subTest(status=status):
                probed = []
                def handler(request):
                    if request.method == "GET":
                        return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model} for model in models]})
                    model = json.loads(request.content)["model"]
                    probed.append(model)
                    if model == models[1]:
                        return httpx.Response(status, json={"error": "Candidate unavailable"})
                    return challenge_reply(request)
                result = discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
                self.assertEqual(result.models, [models[0], models[2]])
                self.assertEqual(probed, models)
                self.assertEqual(result.retired, [])
                self.assertEqual(len(result.notes), 1)
                self.assertIn("Candidate unavailable", result.notes[0])

    def test_all_model_http_errors_return_unverified_notes_without_retiring_generic_410(self):
        models = ["vendor/a-vision", "vendor/b-vision"]

            if request.method == "GET":

    def test_transport_failure_returns_prior_successes_and_stops_remaining_probes(self):
        models = ["vendor/a-vision", "vendor/b-vision", "vendor/c-vision"]

    def test_account_failure_after_success_still_aborts_discovery(self):
        models = ["vendor/a-vision", "vendor/b-vision"]
        for status in (401, 402, 403, 429):
            with self.subTest(status=status):
                def handler(request):
                    if request.method == "GET":
                        return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model} for model in models]})
                    if json.loads(request.content)["model"] == models[0]:

                    discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
                self.assertEqual(caught.exception.status_code, status)

    def test_preferred_embedding_or_incompatible_image_model_cannot_bypass_filter(self):
        valid = "vendor/new-vision"
        excluded_models = ["nvidia/nv-embed-vl", "vendor/vision-embedding-v1",
                           "microsoft/phi-3-vision-128k-instruct", "vendor/plain-text"]

                            for model in [valid, *excluded_models]]})

                    return challenge_reply(request)

                                               transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))

                self.assertEqual(probed, [valid])

    def test_llama_vision_fixed_routes_are_shared_by_analysis_discovery_and_diagnostic(self):
        for size in ("11b", "90b"):
            with self.subTest(size=size):
                model = f"meta/llama-3.2-{size}-vision-instruct"
                endpoint = f"https://ai.api.nvidia.com/v1/gr/{model}/chat/completions"
                calls = []
                def handler(request):
                    calls.append(str(request.url))
                    if request.method == "GET":
                        self.assertEqual(str(request.url), "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1/models")
                        return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model}]})
                    self.assertEqual(str(request.url), endpoint)
                    payload = json.loads(request.content)
                    self.assertEqual(payload["model"], model)
                    if payload["max_tokens"] in (128, 1600):
                        user = next(message for message in payload["messages"] if message["role"] == "user")
                        self.assertEqual([part["type"] for part in user["content"]], ["image_url", "text"])
                        self.assertEqual([message["role"] for message in payload["messages"]], ["user"])
                    if payload["max_tokens"] == 128:
                        return challenge_reply(request)
                    if payload["max_tokens"] == 1600:
                        self.assertIn("주된 촬영 인물", payload["messages"][0]["content"][1]["text"])
                    return reply()
                transport = httpx.MockTransport(handler)
                result = Analyzer("NVIDIA", model, "placeholder", transport=transport).analyze(
                    self.photo, threading.Event())
                self.assertEqual(result["gender"], "남성")
                discovery = discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", transport=transport)
                self.assertEqual(discovery.models, [model])
                report = diagnose_connection("NVIDIA", model, "placeholder", transport=transport)
                self.assertIn("2. 분석 경로: POST " + endpoint, report)
                self.assertIn("텍스트 응답을 받았습니다", report)
                self.assertEqual(calls.count(endpoint), 3)

    def test_model_string_cannot_set_request_destination(self):
        model = "https://untrusted.invalid/steal-key"
        def handler(request):
            self.assertEqual(str(request.url), "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1/chat/completions")

            self.photo, threading.Event())

    def test_exact_reasoning_omni_disables_thinking_with_sufficient_probe_budget(self):
        model = "nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning"
        requests = []
        def handler(request):
            if request.method == "GET":
                return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model}]})
            self.assertEqual(str(request.url), "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1/chat/completions")
            payload = json.loads(request.content)
            requests.append(payload)
            self.assertEqual(payload["chat_template_kwargs"], {"enable_thinking": False})
            if isinstance(payload["messages"][0]["content"], list):
                self.assertEqual(payload["max_tokens"], 1024)
                return challenge_reply(request)
            return reply()
        transport = httpx.MockTransport(handler)
        self.assertEqual(discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", transport=transport).models, [model])
        result = Analyzer("NVIDIA", model, "placeholder", transport=transport).analyze(self.photo, threading.Event())
        self.assertEqual(result["gender"], "남성")
        self.assertEqual(requests[1]["max_tokens"], 1600)
        diagnose_connection("NVIDIA", model, "placeholder", transport=transport)
        self.assertEqual(len(requests), 3)

    def test_gemma4_uses_native_system_role_and_image_before_text_in_analysis_and_probe(self):
        model = "google/gemma-4-31b-it"
        def handler(request):
            if request.method == "GET":
                return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model}]})
            payload = json.loads(request.content)
            self.assertEqual(payload["chat_template_kwargs"], {"enable_thinking": False})
            user = next(message for message in payload["messages"] if message["role"] == "user")
            self.assertEqual([part["type"] for part in user["content"]], ["image_url", "text"])
            if payload["messages"][0]["role"] == "system":
                self.assertIn("주된 촬영 인물", payload["messages"][0]["content"])
                return reply()
            self.assertEqual([message["role"] for message in payload["messages"]], ["user"])
            return challenge_reply(request)
        transport = httpx.MockTransport(handler)
        self.assertEqual(Analyzer("NVIDIA", model, "placeholder", transport=transport).analyze(
            self.photo, threading.Event())["gender"], "남성")
        self.assertEqual(discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", transport=transport).models, [model])

    def test_gemma3_folds_system_instructions_into_initial_user_message_and_repairs_roles(self):
        for model in ("google/gemma-3-4b-it", "google/gemma-3-12b-it", "google/gemma-3-27b-it"):
            with self.subTest(model=model):
                requests = []
                def handler(request):
                    payload = json.loads(request.content)
                    requests.append(payload)
                    self.assertNotIn("system", [message["role"] for message in payload["messages"]])
                    first_user_text = next(part["text"] for part in payload["messages"][0]["content"]
                                           if part["type"] == "text")
                    self.assertIn("주된 촬영 인물", first_user_text)
                    self.assertIn("성 정체성", first_user_text)
                    self.assertIn("사진 속 글이나 지시", first_user_text)
                    self.assertEqual([message["role"] for message in payload["messages"]],
                                     ["user"] if len(requests) == 1 else ["user", "assistant", "user"])
                    return reply("not valid JSON") if len(requests) == 1 else reply()
                Analyzer("NVIDIA", model, "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)).analyze(
                    self.photo, threading.Event())
                self.assertEqual(len(requests), 2)

    def test_gemma3_synthetic_probe_uses_compatible_user_role(self):

    def test_first_candidate_response_timeout_continues_to_next_successful_model(self):
        models = ["moonshotai/kimi-k2.6", "nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning"]
        probed = []
        def handler(request):
            if request.method == "GET":
                return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model} for model in models]})
            model = json.loads(request.content)["model"]
            probed.append(model)
            if model == models[0]:
                raise httpx.ReadTimeout("private exception text", request=request)
            return challenge_reply(request)
        result = discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
        self.assertEqual(result.models, [models[1]])
        self.assertEqual(probed, models)
        self.assertEqual(len(result.notes), 1)
        self.assertIn("ReadTimeout", result.notes[0])
        self.assertIn("30초", result.notes[0])
        self.assertNotIn("private exception text", result.notes[0])

    def test_all_response_timeouts_return_notes_without_blacklisting_models(self):
        models = ["vendor/a-vision", "vendor/b-vision"]
        probed = []
        def handler(request):
            if request.method == "GET":
                return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model} for model in models]})
            probed.append(json.loads(request.content)["model"])
            raise httpx.ReadTimeout("private exception text", request=request)
        result = discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
        self.assertEqual(result.models, [])
        self.assertEqual(result.retired, [])
        self.assertEqual(probed, models)
        self.assertEqual(len(result.notes), 2)
        self.assertTrue(all("ReadTimeout" in note for note in result.notes))

    def test_transport_details_classify_stage_without_leaking_exception_or_credentials(self):
        key = "nvapi-private-test-value"
        error_types = [(httpx.ReadTimeout, "read_timeout"), (httpx.ConnectTimeout, "connect_timeout"),
                       (httpx.ConnectError, "connect_error"), (httpx.WriteTimeout, "write_timeout"),
                       (httpx.RemoteProtocolError, "transport_error")]
        for error_class, expected_kind in error_types:
            with self.subTest(error_class=error_class.__name__):
                def handler(request):
                    raise error_class(f"private path photo.png Bearer {key}", request=request)
                with self.assertRaises(APIError) as caught:
                    _request("POST", "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1/chat/completions", key,
                             threading.Event(), {"model": "vendor/test-vision"},
                             transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler), attempts=1, timeout_seconds=30)
                self.assertEqual(caught.exception.transport_kind, expected_kind)
                message = str(caught.exception)
                self.assertIn("POST https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1/chat/completions", message)
                self.assertIn(error_class.__name__, message)
                self.assertNotIn(key, message)
                self.assertNotIn("photo.png", message)
                self.assertNotIn("private path", message)

    def test_connection_and_write_errors_without_prior_success_stop_discovery(self):
        models = ["vendor/a-vision", "vendor/b-vision"]
        for error_class in (httpx.ConnectError, httpx.ConnectTimeout, httpx.WriteTimeout,
                            httpx.RemoteProtocolError):
            with self.subTest(error_class=error_class.__name__):
                probed = []
                def handler(request):
                    if request.method == "GET":
                        return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model} for model in models]})
                    probed.append(json.loads(request.content)["model"])
                    raise error_class("unavailable", request=request)
                with self.assertRaises(APIError):
                    discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
                self.assertEqual(probed, models[:1])

    def test_cancellation_takes_precedence_over_simultaneous_response_timeout(self):
        cancel = threading.Event()
        def handler(request):
            cancel.set()
            raise httpx.ReadTimeout("deadline", request=request)
        with self.assertRaises(Cancelled):
            _request("POST", "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1/chat/completions", "placeholder",
                     cancel, transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler), attempts=1)

    def test_cancellation_interrupts_model_image_probe(self):
        started, ended, cancel = threading.Event(), threading.Event(), threading.Event()
        results, calls = [], []
        async def handler(request):
            calls.append(request.method)
            if request.method == "GET":
                return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": "vendor/test-vision"}]})
            started.set()
            try:
                await asyncio.sleep(10)
            finally:
                ended.set()
        def run():
            try:
                results.append(discover_photo_models("NVIDIA", "placeholder", cancel=cancel,
                                                     transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)))
            except Exception as error:
                results.append(error)
        worker = threading.Thread(target=run)
        worker.start()
        self.assertTrue(started.wait(1))
        cancel.set()
        worker.join(1)
        self.assertFalse(worker.is_alive())
        self.assertTrue(ended.is_set())
        self.assertIsInstance(results[0], Cancelled)
        self.assertEqual(calls, ["GET", "POST"])

    def test_single_request_has_total_timeout_and_cancels_transport(self):
        ended = threading.Event()
        async def handler(request):
            try:
                await asyncio.sleep(10)
            finally:
                ended.set()
        started = time.monotonic()
        with self.assertRaises(APIError) as caught:
            _request("GET", "https://test.invalid/models", "placeholder", threading.Event(),
                     transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler), attempts=1, timeout_seconds=0.03)
        self.assertEqual(caught.exception.transport_kind, "read_timeout")
        self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - started, 0.5)
        self.assertTrue(ended.is_set())

    def test_catalog_only_diagnostic_preserves_raw_ids_without_inference(self):
        models = ["vendor/text-only", "vendor/new-vision"]
        calls = []
        def handler(request):
            calls.append(request.method)
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"data": [{"id": model} for model in models]})
        report = diagnose_connection("NVIDIA", "", "placeholder", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler))
        self.assertEqual(calls, ["GET"])
        self.assertIn("서버 모델 수: 2", report)
        self.assertIn("vendor/text-only", report)
        self.assertIn("vendor/new-vision", report)
        self.assertIn("미선택", report)
        self.assertIn("생략", report)

    unittest.main()

import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

from frametag.domain import (
    filename_from_analysis, is_gender_prefixed_filename, list_images, natural_key, normalize_analysis,
    parse_frametag_filename, sanitize_filename, select_through, unify_runs,
)
from frametag.models import Photo


def photo(number, gender="남성", objects=None, **flags):
    return Photo(Path(f"/photos/frame{number}.jpg"), 100, 200,
                 {"gender": gender, "clothing": ["검정반팔", "파랑청바지"],
                  "objects": objects or []}, status="완료", **flags)


class AnalysisTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_allowed_clothes_outer_priority_and_blue(self):
        result = normalize_analysis({
            "gender": "male", "clothing": [
                {"color": "black", "type": "t-shirt"},
                {"color": "카키", "type": "조끼"},
                {"color": "navy", "type": "jeans"},
                {"color": "흰색", "type": "긴바지"},
                {"color": "회색", "type": "양복"},
                {"color": "없는색", "type": "우비"},
            ],
        })
        self.assertEqual(result, {"gender": "남성", "clothing": ["카키조끼", "파랑청바지"], "objects": []})

    def test_dress_and_no_person(self):
        raw = {"gender": "여성", "clothing": ["빨강원피스", "파랑청바지", "검정반팔"]}
        self.assertEqual(normalize_analysis(raw)["clothing"], ["빨강원피스"])
        raw["gender"] = "인물없음"
        raw["objects"] = [{"name": "가방", "location": "바닥"}]
        self.assertEqual(normalize_analysis(raw), {"gender": "인물없음", "clothing": [], "objects": []})

    def test_construction_details(self):
        result = normalize_analysis({"shirt": {"type": "반팔", "color": "흰색", "has_collar": True},
                                     "pants": {"type": "긴바지", "color": "진청", "material": "denim"}})
        self.assertEqual(result["clothing"], ["흰색셔츠", "파랑청바지"])

    def test_floor_exclusions_and_bottles(self):
        raw = {"gender": "불명", "objects": [
            {"name": name, "location": "바닥"} for name in ("의자", "종이", "신문", "매트", "신발", "휴대폰")
        ] + [{"name": "휴대폰", "location": "손"}, {"name": "생수병", "location": "floor"},
             {"name": "glass bottle", "location": "hand"}, {"name": "우산", "location": "unknown"}]}
        self.assertEqual(normalize_analysis(raw)["objects"], ["병(손)", "휴대폰(손)", "페트병(바닥)"])


class FilenameTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_generated_and_manual_object_rules(self):
        analysis = {"gender": "남성", "clothing": ["검정반팔", "파랑청바지"],
                    "objects": ["가방(손)", "캐리어(바닥)"]}
        self.assertEqual(filename_from_analysis(analysis, ".jpg"),
                         "남성_검정반팔_파랑청바지_손-가방_바닥-캐리어.jpg")
        analysis.update(clothing=["내가 쓴 옷"], objects=["의자(바닥)", "종이", "우산"])
        self.assertEqual(filename_from_analysis(analysis, ".JPG"), "남성_내가쓴옷_바닥-의자_종이_우산.JPG")

    def test_manual_names_never_escape_folder_or_change_format(self):
        for name in ("../outside", r"..\outside", "C:outside", "AUX", "con.txt", "CON .txt", "CONIN$", "LPT¹", "...", "x ", "x.", "x\x00", "x.png"):
            with self.subTest(name=name), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                sanitize_filename(name, ".jpg")
        self.assertEqual(sanitize_filename("새 사진", ".jpg"), "새 사진.jpg")
        self.assertEqual(sanitize_filename("새 사진.JPG", ".jpg"), "새 사진.jpg")

    def test_generated_input_is_safe_and_long_korean_is_bounded(self):
        result = filename_from_analysis({"gender": "CON", "clothing": ["옷" * 500],
                                         "objects": ["../outside(손)"]}, ".png")
        self.assertLessEqual(len(result.encode("utf-8")), 240)
        self.assertEqual(Path(result).suffix, ".png")
        self.assertNotIn("/", result)
        self.assertEqual(sanitize_filename(result, ".png"), result)


class GenderPrefixExclusionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_all_gender_prefixes_ignore_clothing_objects_and_collision_style(self):
        names = (
            "여성_교복_검정자켓.jpg", "여성_교복_곰인형.jpg",
            "여성_교복_아크릴판.jpg", "여성_교복_유리판_6.jpg",
            "여성_꽃무늬원피스_대걸레 (1).jpg",
            "여성_검정스트라이프긴팔_베이지바지.jpg",
            "남성+여성_회색반팔_파랑청바지.jpg", "여성+남성_교복_2.PNG",
            "남성_군복.jpg", "남성_군복_가방_4.jpg", "여성_휴가사진.png",
            "남성사진.JPG", "여성.jpg",
        )
        for name in names:
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                self.assertTrue(is_gender_prefixed_filename(name))

    def test_labels_inside_original_names_or_proposed_names_do_not_exclude(self):
        for name in ("IMG_남성.jpg", "사진_여성_교복.jpg", "original.png", "1 (2).jpg", ""):
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                self.assertFalse(is_gender_prefixed_filename(name))
        original = photo(1)
        original.proposed_name = "남성_군복.jpg"
        self.assertEqual(select_through([original], original), [original])

    def test_prefix_exclusion_splits_unification_runs_and_selection(self):
        photos = [photo(1), photo(2), photo(3, objects=["가방(손)"])]
        photos[1].path = Path("/photos/남성+여성_군복.jpg")
        original_analysis = [dict(item.analysis) for item in photos]
        self.assertEqual(unify_runs(photos), [])
        self.assertEqual([item.analysis for item in photos], original_analysis)
        self.assertEqual(select_through(photos, photos[2]), [photos[0], photos[2]])
        self.assertEqual(select_through(photos, photos[1]), [])


class ExistingFrameTagNameTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_actual_existing_photo_names_keep_clothes_without_collision_number(self):
        cases = {
            "여성_흰색반팔_검정긴바지_3.jpg": {
                "gender": "여성", "clothing": ["흰색반팔", "검정긴바지"], "objects": []},
            "남성_흰색반팔_검정반바지_16.JPG": {
                "gender": "남성", "clothing": ["흰색반팔", "검정반바지"], "objects": []},
        }
        for name, expected in cases.items():
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                self.assertEqual(parse_frametag_filename(name), expected)

    def test_documented_filename_and_legacy_collision_styles(self):
        expected = {"gender": "남성", "clothing": ["검정반팔", "파랑청바지"],
                    "objects": ["가방(손)", "캐리어(바닥)"]}
        for ending in (".jpg", "_2.JPG", "_16.jpeg", "_(2).png", " (2).webp"):
            name = "남성_검정반팔_파랑청바지_손-가방_바닥-캐리어" + ending
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                self.assertEqual(parse_frametag_filename(name), expected)

    def test_dress_bottom_only_and_known_colors(self):
        cases = {
            "여성_빨강원피스.jpg": ["빨강원피스"],
            "불명_베이지멜빵바지.png": ["베이지멜빵바지"],
            "남성_카키조끼_회색레깅스.gif": ["카키조끼", "회색레깅스"],
            "여성_다색체크셔츠_분홍치마.jpeg": ["다색체크셔츠", "분홍치마"],
            "남성_주황우비_갈색긴바지.webp": ["주황우비", "갈색긴바지"],
            "불명_보라후드_초록반바지.JPG": ["보라후드", "초록반바지"],
        }
        for name, expected in cases.items():
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                self.assertEqual(parse_frametag_filename(name)["clothing"], expected)

    def test_manual_objects_and_location_are_preserved_without_ai_exclusions(self):
        name = "여성_노랑긴팔_파랑청바지_우산_상자_바닥-의자_바닥-종이.jpg"
        self.assertEqual(parse_frametag_filename(name), {
            "gender": "여성", "clothing": ["노랑긴팔", "파랑청바지"],
            "objects": ["우산", "상자", "의자(바닥)", "종이(바닥)"],
        })

    def test_explicit_objects_are_recognized_when_clothes_are_unavailable(self):
        self.assertEqual(parse_frametag_filename("남성_손-휴대폰_바닥-가방.jpg"), {
            "gender": "남성", "clothing": [], "objects": ["휴대폰(손)", "가방(바닥)"],
        })

    def test_exact_uncertain_and_no_person_output_without_clothes(self):
        for name in ("불명.jpg", "인물없음.JPG", "불명_2.png", "인물없음 (3).gif"):
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                self.assertEqual(parse_frametag_filename(name), {
                    "gender": name.split(".")[0].split("_")[0].split(" ")[0],
                    "clothing": [], "objects": [],
                })

    def test_original_and_arbitrary_manual_names_are_not_guessed(self):
        for name in ("IMG_20261011_123456.jpg", "1 (2).jpg", "사진.jpg",
                     "여성.jpg", "남성.jpg", "여성_참고.jpg", "남성_검정옷.jpg",
                     "여성_원피스.jpg", "불명_참고.jpg", "여성_우산_상자.jpg",
                     "여성_흰색반팔_검정긴바지.txt"):
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                self.assertIsNone(parse_frametag_filename(name))

    def test_malformed_structured_names_are_not_guessed(self):
        for name in ("남성_검정반팔__파랑청바지.jpg", "남성_없는색반팔.jpg",
                     "여성_파랑청바지_검정반팔.jpg", "여성_빨강원피스_검정치마.jpg",
                     "남성_검정반팔_흰색셔츠.jpg", "남성_검정반팔_파랑청바지_검정조끼.jpg",
                     "남성_손-.jpg", "남성_검정반팔_손-가방_.jpg",
                     "../남성_검정반팔.jpg", r"folder\남성_검정반팔.jpg",
                     "남성_검정반팔_손:가방.jpg"):
            with self.subTest(name=name):
                self.assertIsNone(parse_frametag_filename(name))


class SequenceTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_majority_objects_and_gender(self):
        photos = [photo(1, objects=["가방(손)"]), photo(2), photo(3, objects=["가방(손)"])]
        self.assertEqual(unify_runs(photos), [photos[1]])
        self.assertEqual(photos[1].analysis["objects"], ["가방(손)"])
        self.assertEqual(photos[1].revision, 1)
        self.assertEqual(photos[1].status, "완료")
        photos = [photo(1, "불명"), photo(2, "여성"), photo(3, "여성")]
        self.assertEqual(unify_runs(photos), [photos[0]])
        self.assertEqual(photos[0].analysis["gender"], "여성")

    def test_ties_prefer_more_objects_and_known_gender(self):
        photos = [photo(1), photo(2, objects=["가방(손)"]), photo(3, objects=["가방(손)", "우산(손)"])]
        unify_runs(photos)
        self.assertTrue(all(p.analysis["objects"] == ["가방(손)", "우산(손)"] for p in photos))
        photos = [photo(1, "불명"), photo(2, "남성"), photo(3, "여성")]
        unify_runs(photos)
        self.assertTrue(all(p.analysis["gender"] == "남성" for p in photos))

    def test_manual_confirmed_and_folder_boundaries(self):
        for flag in ("manual", "confirmed"):
            photos = [photo(1), photo(2, objects=["우산(손)"], **{flag: True}), photo(3)]
            self.assertEqual(unify_runs(photos), [])
            self.assertEqual(photos[1].analysis["objects"], ["우산(손)"])
        photos = [photo(1), photo(2), photo(3, objects=["우산(손)"])]
        photos[2].path = Path("/other/frame3.jpg")
        self.assertEqual(unify_runs(photos), [])

    def test_select_through_excludes_already_confirmed(self):
        photos = [photo(1, confirmed=True), photo(2), photo(3), photo(4)]
        self.assertEqual(select_through(photos, photos[2]), photos[1:3])
        self.assertEqual(select_through(photos, photos[0]), [])
        self.assertEqual(select_through(photos, photo(8)), [])


class ImageListTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_natural_sort_supported_formats_and_recursion(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as folder:
            root = Path(folder)
            for name in ("photo10.JPG", "photo2.png", "photo1.jpg", "note.txt", "photo3.gif"):
                (root / name).touch()
            (root / "sub").mkdir()
            (root / "sub" / "photo1.webp").touch()
            self.assertEqual([p.name for p in list_images(root)], ["photo1.jpg", "photo2.png", "photo3.gif", "photo10.JPG"])
            self.assertEqual(len(list_images(root, recursive=True)), 5)
            self.assertLess(natural_key("pic2"), natural_key("pic10"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

import io
import json
import tempfile
import threading
import time
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

from PIL import Image

from frametag.api import APIError, Cancelled
from frametag.engine import Engine
from frametag.models import Photo
from frametag.rename import _move_no_clobber
from frametag.storage import Cache


RESULT = {"gender": "남성", "clothing": ["검정반팔", "파랑청바지"],
          "objects": [{"name": "가방", "location": "손"}]}
TAGGED_NAME = "여성_흰색반팔_검정반바지_손-종이 (2).png"
TAGGED_ANALYSIS = {"gender": "여성", "clothing": ["흰색반팔", "검정반바지"],
                   "objects": ["종이(손)"]}


def wait_until(predicate, timeout=3.0):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if predicate():
            return
        time.sleep(0.01)
    raise AssertionError("background operation did not reach expected state")


class FakeAPI:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = []
        self.entered = threading.Event()
        self.release = threading.Event()
        self.release.set()
        self.failure = None

    def factory(self, provider, model, key):
        outer = self

        class Analyzer:
            def analyze(self, path, cancel):
                outer.calls.append((path.name, provider, model))
                outer.entered.set()
                while not outer.release.wait(0.01):
                    if cancel.is_set():
                        raise Cancelled()
                if cancel.is_set():
                    raise Cancelled()
                if outer.failure:
                    raise outer.failure
                return RESULT

        return Analyzer()


class EngineTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)
        self.events = []
        self.api = FakeAPI()
        self.engines = []
        self.addCleanup(self.close_all)

    def close_all(self):
        self.api.release.set()
        for engine in self.engines:
            engine.shutdown()

    def engine(self, interval=0.03, analyzer_factory=None):
        engine = Engine(lambda kind, value: self.events.append((kind, value)),
                        interval=interval, stable_scans=3,
                        analyzer_factory=analyzer_factory or self.api.factory)
        self.engines.append(engine)
        engine.configure("OpenAI", "fake-model", "test-placeholder")
        engine.load_folder(self.root)
        return engine

    def photo(self, name="original.png", color="red"):
        path = self.root / name
        Image.new("RGB", (16, 16), color).save(path)
        return path

    def idle(self, engine):
        wait_until(lambda: not engine._busy)

    def _blocked_key_failure(self, old_key):
        calls = []
        self.api.release.clear()

        def factory(provider, model, key):
            outer = self

            class Analyzer:
                def analyze(self, path, cancel):
                    calls.append((path.name, key))
                    if key == old_key:
                        outer.api.entered.set()
                        while not outer.api.release.wait(.01):
                            if cancel.is_set():
                                raise Cancelled()
                        raise APIError("Old configuration returned HTTP 410", fatal=True)
                    return RESULT

            return Analyzer()

        return factory, calls

    def test_existing_tagged_filename_is_cached_confirmed_without_rename_history(self):
        path = self.photo(TAGGED_NAME)
        before = path.read_bytes()
        engine = self.engine()
        photo = engine.get_photos()[0]
        self.assertEqual(photo.analysis, TAGGED_ANALYSIS)
        self.assertEqual(photo.proposed_name, TAGGED_NAME)
        self.assertTrue(photo.confirmed)
        self.assertFalse(photo.manual)
        self.assertEqual(photo.status, "기존 이름 변경 완료")
        self.assertEqual(photo.model, "")
        self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), before)
        cached = Cache(self.root).get(path)
        self.assertTrue(cached.confirmed)
        self.assertEqual(cached.analysis, TAGGED_ANALYSIS)
        record = json.loads((self.root / ".frametag_watch_v2.json").read_text())
        self.assertEqual(record["entries"][TAGGED_NAME]["state"], "processed")
        self.assertFalse((self.root / "frametag_history_v2.json").exists())
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.idle(engine)
        engine.set_auto(True)
        time.sleep(0.15)
        self.assertEqual(self.api.calls, [])

    def test_manual_analysis_processes_original_only_in_mixed_folder(self):
        tagged = self.photo(TAGGED_NAME)
        original = self.photo("original.png")
        arbitrary = self.photo("여성_휴가사진.png")
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.idle(engine)
        self.assertEqual({call[0] for call in self.api.calls},
                         {original.name})
        photos = {photo.path.name: photo for photo in engine.get_photos()}
        self.assertTrue(photos[tagged.name].confirmed)
        self.assertFalse(photos[original.name].confirmed)
        self.assertFalse(photos[arbitrary.name].confirmed)
        self.assertFalse(engine._needs_analysis(photos[arbitrary.name]))
        self.assertEqual(engine._known[arbitrary.name]["state"], "processed")
        self.assertTrue(tagged.exists())

    def test_all_user_gender_prefixes_skip_analysis_without_changing_files(self):
        names = [
            "여성_교복_검정자켓.jpg", "여성_교복_검정자켓_2.jpg",
            "여성_교복_곰인형.jpg", "여성_교복_아크릴판.jpg",
            "여성_교복_유리판_6.jpg", "여성_교복_책_3.jpg",
            "여성_교복_페트병.jpg", "여성_꽃무늬원피스_대걸레 (1).jpg",
            "여성_검정스트라이프긴팔_베이지긴바지.jpg",
            "남성+여성_회색반팔_파랑청바지.jpg", "남성_군복.jpg",
            "남성_군복_가방_2.jpg", "여성+남성_직접정한의상.jpg",
            "여성사진.jpg", "남성.jpg", "여성.png",
        ]
        paths = [self.photo(name) for name in names]
        before = {path.name: path.read_bytes() for path in paths}
        original = self.photo("IMG_20261011.jpg")
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.idle(engine)
        engine.set_auto(True)
        wait_until(lambda: all(engine._stable[name][1] >= 3 for name in names))
        self.assertEqual([call[0] for call in self.api.calls], [original.name])
        photos = {photo.path.name: photo for photo in engine.get_photos()}
        self.assertTrue(all(not engine._needs_analysis(photos[name]) for name in names))
        self.assertTrue(all(engine._known[name]["state"] == "processed" for name in names))
        self.assertEqual({path.name: path.read_bytes() for path in paths}, before)
        self.assertFalse((self.root / "frametag_history_v2.json").exists())

    def test_gender_prefix_overrides_manifest_states_and_preserves_cached_metadata(self):
        names = ["여성_교복_곰인형.jpg", "남성+여성_직접정한의상.jpg",
                 "여성_검정스트라이프긴팔_베이지긴바지.jpg", "남성_군복_가방_2.jpg"]
        originals = []
        cache = Cache(self.root)
        for index, name in enumerate(names):
            path = self.photo(name)
            photo = Photo.from_path(path)
            if index == 1:
                photo.status, photo.error, photo.model = "오류", "이전 API 오류", "이전 모델"
            elif index == 2:
                photo.analysis = {"gender": "불명", "clothing": ["직접정한옷"], "objects": ["우산"]}
                photo.status, photo.proposed_name, photo.model = "분석 완료", "불명_직접정한옷_우산.jpg", "이전 모델"
            elif index == 3:
                photo.manual, photo.revision, photo.status = True, 4, "수동 수정"
                photo.analysis = {"gender": "남성", "clothing": ["군복"], "objects": ["가방"]}
                photo.proposed_name = "직접변경예정.jpg"
            cache.put(photo)
            originals.append(photo)
        states = ["pending", "error", "baseline", "pending"]
        (self.root / ".frametag_watch_v2.json").write_text(json.dumps({
            "version": 2, "recursive": False, "entries": {
                photo.path.name: {"state": state, "size": photo.size,
                                  "mtime_ns": photo.mtime_ns, "present": True}
                for photo, state in zip(originals, states)}}), encoding="utf-8")
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.idle(engine)
        engine.set_auto(True)
        engine.configure("OpenAI", "changed-model", "changed-placeholder")
        wait_until(lambda: all(engine._stable[name][1] >= 3 for name in names))
        self.assertEqual(self.api.calls, [])
        photos = {photo.path.name: photo for photo in engine.get_photos()}
        for original in originals:
            with self.subTest(name=original.path.name):
                self.assertEqual(photos[original.path.name], original)
                self.assertEqual(cache.get(original.path), original)
                self.assertEqual(engine._known[original.path.name]["state"], "processed")
        record = json.loads((self.root / ".frametag_watch_v2.json").read_text())
        self.assertTrue(all(record["entries"][name]["state"] == "processed" for name in names))
        self.assertFalse((self.root / "frametag_history_v2.json").exists())

    def test_new_gender_prefixes_are_skipped_while_new_original_is_renamed_once(self):
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.set_auto(True)
        names = ["여성_교복_유리판_4.jpg", "남성+여성_회색반팔_파랑청바지.jpg",
                 "남성_군복_가방_2.jpg"]
        paths = [self.photo(name) for name in names]
        before = {path.name: path.read_bytes() for path in paths}
        original = self.photo("new_original.jpg", color="blue")
        contents = original.read_bytes()
        wait_until(lambda: len(self.api.calls) == 1 and not engine.is_busy()
                   and not original.exists() and len(engine.get_photos()) == 4)
        wait_until(lambda: all(engine._stable[name][1] >= 3 for name in names))
        self.assertEqual([call[0] for call in self.api.calls], [original.name])
        self.assertEqual({path.name: path.read_bytes() for path in paths}, before)
        self.assertTrue(all(engine._known[name]["state"] == "processed" for name in names))
        self.assertIn(contents, [photo.path.read_bytes() for photo in engine.get_photos()])
        history = json.loads((self.root / "frametag_history_v2.json").read_text())
        self.assertEqual(len(history), 1)
        self.assertEqual(history[0]["entries"][0]["source"], original.name)

    def test_direct_process_does_not_analyze_or_rename_gender_prefixed_photo(self):
        path = self.photo("여성_교복_곰인형.jpg")
        contents = path.read_bytes()
        engine = self.engine()
        engine._process(str(path), automatic=False)
        engine._process(str(path), automatic=True)
        self.assertEqual(self.api.calls, [])
        self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), contents)
        self.assertFalse((self.root / "frametag_history_v2.json").exists())

    def test_gender_prefix_cached_api_proposal_requires_manual_edit_before_apply(self):
        path = self.photo("여성_교복_곰인형.jpg")
        contents = path.read_bytes()
        cached = Photo.from_path(path)
        cached.analysis = {"gender": "불명", "clothing": [], "objects": []}
        cached.proposed_name, cached.status = "불명.jpg", "분석 완료"
        Cache(self.root).put(cached)
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.apply_selected([str(path)])
        self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), contents)
        self.assertFalse((self.root / cached.proposed_name).exists())
        self.assertFalse((self.root / "frametag_history_v2.json").exists())
        self.assertEqual(engine.get_photos()[0], cached)
        engine.update_photo(str(path), "proposed_name", "직접확인.jpg")
        engine.apply_selected([str(path)])
        self.assertFalse(path.exists())
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "직접확인.jpg").read_bytes(), contents)
        self.assertEqual(self.api.calls, [])

    def test_gender_prefix_preserves_explicit_manual_apply_undo_and_selection(self):
        path = self.photo("남성_군복_가방_2.jpg")
        original = self.photo("original.png")
        contents = path.read_bytes()
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.update_photo(str(path), "proposed_name", "직접수정.jpg")
        self.assertEqual(engine.select_through(str(path)), [])
        self.assertEqual(engine.select_through(str(original)), [str(original)])
        engine.apply_selected([str(path)])
        target = self.root / "직접수정.jpg"
        self.assertEqual(target.read_bytes(), contents)
        self.assertFalse(path.exists())
        engine.undo()
        self.assertEqual(path.read_bytes(), contents)
        restored = {photo.path.name: photo for photo in engine.get_photos()}[path.name]
        self.assertTrue(restored.manual)
        self.assertFalse(restored.confirmed)
        self.assertEqual(restored.proposed_name, target.name)
        self.assertFalse(engine._needs_analysis(restored))
        engine.shutdown()
        restarted = self.engine()
        restarted.set_auto(True)
        wait_until(lambda: restarted._stable[path.name][1] >= 3)
        self.assertEqual(self.api.calls, [])
        self.assertEqual({photo.path.name: photo for photo in restarted.get_photos()}[path.name], restored)
        self.assertEqual(restarted._known[path.name]["state"], "processed")

    def test_user_numbered_tagged_names_skip_analysis_and_keep_existing_baseline(self):
        names = ["여성_흰색반팔_검정긴바지_3.jpg",
                 "남성_흰색반팔_검정반바지_16.jpg"]
        paths = [self.photo(name) for name in names]
        (self.root / ".frametag_watch_v2.json").write_text(json.dumps({
            "version": 2, "recursive": False, "entries": {
                path.name: {"state": "baseline", "size": path.stat().st_size,
                            "mtime_ns": path.stat().st_mtime_ns, "present": True}
                for path in paths}}), encoding="utf-8")
        engine = self.engine()
        self.assertTrue(all(photo.confirmed for photo in engine.get_photos()))
        self.assertTrue(all(engine._known[name]["state"] == "processed" for name in names))
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.idle(engine)
        engine.set_auto(True)
        time.sleep(0.15)
        self.assertEqual(self.api.calls, [])
        self.assertTrue(all(path.exists() for path in paths))
        for path in paths:
            self.assertTrue(Cache(self.root).get(path).confirmed)

    def test_arrival_is_visible_during_slow_auto_analysis_and_waits_for_complete_copy(self):
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.set_auto(True)
        self.api.release.clear()
        first = self.photo("first.png")
        self.assertTrue(self.api.entered.wait(2))
        buffer = io.BytesIO()
        Image.new("RGB", (48, 32), "blue").save(buffer, format="PNG")
        contents = buffer.getvalue()
        incoming = self.root / "incoming.png"
        incoming.write_bytes(contents[:20])
        wait_until(lambda: len(engine.get_photos()) == 2)
        photos = {photo.path.name: photo for photo in engine.get_photos()}
        self.assertTrue(engine.is_busy())
        self.assertEqual(photos[incoming.name].status, "대기")
        self.assertEqual(engine._known[incoming.name]["state"], "pending")
        self.assertEqual([call[0] for call in self.api.calls], [first.name])
        self.api.release.set()
        wait_until(lambda: any(photo.confirmed for photo in engine.get_photos()))
        self.idle(engine)
        time.sleep(0.12)
        self.assertEqual([call[0] for call in self.api.calls], [first.name])
        incoming.write_bytes(contents)
        wait_until(lambda: len(engine.get_photos()) == 2
                   and all(photo.confirmed for photo in engine.get_photos()))
        self.assertEqual([call[0] for call in self.api.calls], [first.name, incoming.name])
        self.assertFalse(incoming.exists())
        self.assertIn(contents, [photo.path.read_bytes() for photo in engine.get_photos()])

    def test_arrival_during_manual_batch_is_visible_and_processed_after_batch(self):
        originals = [self.photo("first.png"), self.photo("second.png")]
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.set_auto(True)
        self.api.release.clear()
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.assertTrue(self.api.entered.wait(2))
        incoming = self.photo("incoming.png", color="blue")
        wait_until(lambda: len(engine.get_photos()) == 3)
        self.assertTrue(engine.is_busy())
        self.assertEqual([call[0] for call in self.api.calls], [originals[0].name])
        self.api.release.set()
        wait_until(lambda: len(self.api.calls) == 3 and not engine.is_busy())
        self.assertEqual([call[0] for call in self.api.calls],
                         [originals[0].name, originals[1].name, incoming.name])
        self.assertTrue(all(path.exists() for path in originals))
        self.assertFalse(incoming.exists())
        self.assertEqual(sum(photo.confirmed for photo in engine.get_photos()), 1)

    def test_arrival_during_rename_transaction_is_scanned_after_commit_once(self):
        original = self.photo()
        original_contents = original.read_bytes()
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.set_auto(True)
        engine.update_photo(str(original), "proposed_name", "reviewed.png")
        staged, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
        blocked = False

        def pause_after_first_move(source, target, entry):
            nonlocal blocked
            _move_no_clobber(source, target, entry)
            if not blocked:
                blocked = True
                staged.set()
                if not release.wait(3):
                    raise AssertionError("rename was not released")

        operation = threading.Thread(target=engine.apply_selected,
                                     args=([str(original)],), daemon=True)
        with patch("frametag.rename._move_no_clobber", side_effect=pause_after_first_move):
            operation.start()
            try:
                self.assertTrue(staged.wait(2))
                incoming = self.photo("incoming.png", color="blue")
                incoming_contents = incoming.read_bytes()
                time.sleep(0.12)
                self.assertFalse(original.exists())  # Already staged to the temporary name.
                self.assertEqual(self.api.calls, [])
                # Scanning waits for the transaction lock; transient staging
                # state must never publish a disappearing original or temp row.
                snapshots = [value for kind, value in self.events if kind == "photos"]
                self.assertEqual([photo.path.name for photo in snapshots[-1]], [original.name])
            finally:
                release.set()
                operation.join(3)
            self.assertFalse(operation.is_alive())
            wait_until(lambda: len(engine.get_photos()) == 2
                       and all(photo.confirmed for photo in engine.get_photos()))
        self.assertEqual([call[0] for call in self.api.calls], [incoming.name])
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "reviewed.png").read_bytes(), original_contents)
        self.assertIn(incoming_contents, [photo.path.read_bytes() for photo in engine.get_photos()])
        history = json.loads((self.root / "frametag_history_v2.json").read_text())
        self.assertEqual(len(history), 2)
        self.assertEqual(history[0]["entries"][0]["source"], original.name)
        self.assertEqual(history[0]["entries"][0]["target"], "reviewed.png")
        self.assertEqual(history[1]["entries"][0]["source"], incoming.name)
        self.assertFalse((self.root / ".frametag_pending_v2.json").exists())
        self.assertEqual(list(self.root.glob(".frametag-*.tmp")), [])

    def test_auto_disabled_still_queues_arrival_during_manual_analysis(self):
        original = self.photo()
        engine = self.engine()
        self.api.release.clear()
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.assertTrue(self.api.entered.wait(2))
        incoming = self.photo("incoming.png")
        wait_until(lambda: len(engine.get_photos()) == 2)
        self.assertEqual(engine._known[incoming.name]["state"], "pending")
        self.assertTrue(engine.is_busy())
        self.api.release.set()
        self.idle(engine)
        time.sleep(0.12)
        self.assertEqual([call[0] for call in self.api.calls], [original.name])
        self.assertTrue(incoming.exists())
        photos = {photo.path.name: photo for photo in engine.get_photos()}
        self.assertEqual(photos[incoming.name].status, "대기")
        self.assertIsNone(photos[incoming.name].analysis)
        engine.set_auto(True)
        wait_until(lambda: any(photo.confirmed for photo in engine.get_photos()))
        self.assertEqual([call[0] for call in self.api.calls], [original.name, incoming.name])

    def test_shutdown_cancels_request_and_stops_worker_and_watcher(self):
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.set_auto(True)
        self.api.release.clear()
        self.photo("first.png")
        self.assertTrue(self.api.entered.wait(2))
        self.photo("incoming.png")
        wait_until(lambda: len(engine.get_photos()) == 2)
        engine.shutdown()
        self.assertFalse(engine._worker.is_alive())
        self.assertFalse(engine._watcher.is_alive())
        self.assertEqual([call[0] for call in self.api.calls], ["first.png"])
        record = json.loads((self.root / ".frametag_watch_v2.json").read_text())
        self.assertEqual(record["entries"]["incoming.png"]["state"], "pending")

    def test_stale_request_error_does_not_poison_watcher_replaced_file(self):
        api = self.api

        def factory(provider, model, key):
            delegate = api.factory(provider, model, key)

            class Analyzer:
                def analyze(self, path, cancel):
                    first_call = not api.calls
                    result = delegate.analyze(path, cancel)
                    if first_call:
                        raise APIError("이전 파일 요청 오류", fatal=True)
                    return result

            return Analyzer()

        engine = self.engine(analyzer_factory=factory)
        engine.set_auto(True)
        api.release.clear()
        self.photo("first.png")
        self.assertTrue(api.entered.wait(2))
        previous = engine.get_photos()[0]
        self.photo("first.png", color="blue")
        wait_until(lambda: (engine.get_photos()[0].size, engine.get_photos()[0].mtime_ns)
                   != (previous.size, previous.mtime_ns))
        self.assertEqual(engine.get_photos()[0].status, "대기")
        api.release.set()
        wait_until(lambda: engine.get_photos()[0].confirmed)
        self.assertEqual([call[0] for call in api.calls], ["first.png", "first.png"])
        self.assertEqual(engine.get_photos()[0].error, "")
        self.assertTrue(engine._auto)

    def test_new_tagged_photo_skips_auto_while_original_is_processed(self):

    def test_tagged_existing_manifest_baseline_is_updated_to_processed(self):
        path = self.photo(TAGGED_NAME)
        stat = path.stat()
        (self.root / ".frametag_watch_v2.json").write_text(json.dumps({
            "version": 2, "recursive": False, "entries": {
                TAGGED_NAME: {"state": "baseline", "size": stat.st_size,
                              "mtime_ns": stat.st_mtime_ns, "present": True}}}),
            encoding="utf-8")
        engine = self.engine()
        self.assertTrue(engine.get_photos()[0].confirmed)
        self.assertEqual(engine._known[TAGGED_NAME]["state"], "processed")
        record = json.loads((self.root / ".frametag_watch_v2.json").read_text())
        self.assertEqual(record["entries"][TAGGED_NAME]["state"], "processed")

    def test_valid_unconfirmed_cache_is_preserved_but_gender_prefix_skips_analysis(self):
        path = self.photo(TAGGED_NAME)
        cached = Photo.from_path(path)
        # A valid awaiting-analysis record can come from an earlier version or
        # an undo. Keep that metadata, while the gender prefix excludes analysis.
        Cache(self.root).put(cached)
        engine = self.engine()
        self.assertFalse(engine.get_photos()[0].confirmed)
        self.assertIsNone(engine.get_photos()[0].analysis)
        self.assertEqual(engine.get_photos()[0].status, "대기")
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.idle(engine)
        self.assertEqual(self.api.calls, [])
        self.assertFalse(engine.get_photos()[0].confirmed)
        self.assertEqual(engine.get_photos()[0], cached)
        self.assertEqual(Cache(self.root).get(path), cached)
        self.assertEqual(engine._known[TAGGED_NAME]["state"], "processed")

    def test_manual_edit_of_recognized_photo_survives_reload_and_undo(self):
        path = self.photo(TAGGED_NAME)
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.update_photo(str(path), "clothing", "직접정한의상")
        engine.shutdown()
        restarted = self.engine()
        photo = restarted.get_photos()[0]
        self.assertFalse(photo.confirmed)
        self.assertTrue(photo.manual)
        self.assertEqual(photo.analysis["clothing"], ["직접정한의상"])
        restarted.start_analysis()
        self.idle(restarted)
        self.assertEqual(self.api.calls, [])
        restarted.apply_selected([str(path)])
        self.assertTrue(restarted.get_photos()[0].confirmed)
        restarted.undo()
        self.assertTrue(path.exists())
        self.assertFalse(restarted.get_photos()[0].confirmed)
        restarted.shutdown()
        reloaded = self.engine()
        self.assertFalse(reloaded.get_photos()[0].confirmed)
        self.assertEqual(reloaded.get_photos()[0].analysis["clothing"], ["직접정한의상"])

    def test_replaced_tagged_file_ignores_stale_manual_cache_and_skips_api(self):
        path = self.photo(TAGGED_NAME)
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.update_photo(str(path), "clothing", "이전사진의상")
        self.assertTrue(engine.get_photos()[0].manual)
        self.photo(TAGGED_NAME, color="blue")
        wait_until(lambda: engine.get_photos()[0].confirmed)
        self.assertFalse(engine.get_photos()[0].manual)
        self.assertEqual(engine.get_photos()[0].analysis, TAGGED_ANALYSIS)
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.idle(engine)
        self.assertEqual(self.api.calls, [])

    def test_copy_waits_for_stability_and_decodable_contents(self):

    def test_recursive_scope_expansion_baselines_existing_subfolder_photos(self):
        subfolder = self.root / "subfolder"
        subfolder.mkdir()
        existing = subfolder / "existing.png"
        Image.new("RGB", (16, 16), "red").save(existing)
        engine = self.engine()
        self.assertTrue(engine.load_folder(self.root, recursive=True))
        engine.set_auto(True)
        time.sleep(0.15)
        self.assertEqual(self.api.calls, [])
        self.assertTrue(existing.exists())
        added = subfolder / "new.png"
        Image.new("RGB", (16, 16), "blue").save(added)
        wait_until(lambda: any(p.confirmed for p in engine.get_photos()))
        self.assertEqual([call[0] for call in self.api.calls], ["new.png"])

    def test_scope_expansion_preserves_known_pending_and_top_level_offline_arrivals(self):
        subfolder = self.root / "subfolder"
        subfolder.mkdir()
        engine = self.engine()
        self.assertTrue(engine.load_folder(self.root, recursive=True))
        nested = subfolder / "waiting.png"
        Image.new("RGB", (16, 16), "red").save(nested)
        wait_until(lambda: len(engine.get_photos()) == 1)
        self.assertTrue(engine.load_folder(self.root, recursive=False))
        engine.shutdown()
        self.photo("offline.png")
        restarted = self.engine()
        self.assertTrue(restarted.load_folder(self.root, recursive=True))
        restarted.set_auto(True)
        wait_until(lambda: len(self.api.calls) == 2 and not restarted._busy)
        self.assertEqual({call[0] for call in self.api.calls}, {"offline.png", "waiting.png"})
