    def test_old_key_failure_retries_manual_photo_with_new_key_and_finishes_batch(self):
        self.photo("first.png")
        self.photo("second.png")
        old_key, new_key = "old-key-placeholder", "new-key-placeholder"
        factory, calls = self._blocked_key_failure(old_key)
        engine = self.engine(analyzer_factory=factory)
        engine.configure("OpenAI", "fake-model", old_key)
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.assertTrue(self.api.entered.wait(2))
        engine.configure("OpenAI", "fake-model", new_key)
        self.api.release.set()
        self.idle(engine)
        self.assertEqual(calls, [("first.png", old_key), ("first.png", new_key),
                                 ("second.png", new_key)])
        self.assertTrue(all(photo.analysis and not photo.error
                            and photo.status == "분석 완료" for photo in engine.get_photos()))
        self.assertEqual(engine._consecutive_failures, 0)
        self.assertFalse(any(kind == "error" for kind, _value in self.events))
        snapshots = [value for kind, value in self.events if kind == "photos"]
        self.assertTrue(any(photos[0].status == "대기" for photos in snapshots[1:]))

    def test_old_key_failure_keeps_auto_enabled_and_renames_arrivals_with_new_key(self):
        old_key, new_key = "old-key-placeholder", "new-key-placeholder"
        factory, calls = self._blocked_key_failure(old_key)
        engine = self.engine(analyzer_factory=factory)
        engine.configure("OpenAI", "fake-model", old_key)
        engine.set_auto(True)
        first = self.photo("first.png")
        first_contents = first.read_bytes()
        self.assertTrue(self.api.entered.wait(2))
        engine.configure("OpenAI", "fake-model", new_key)
        second = self.photo("second.png", color="blue")
        second_contents = second.read_bytes()
        self.api.release.set()
        wait_until(lambda: len(engine.get_photos()) == 2
                   and all(photo.confirmed for photo in engine.get_photos())
                   and not engine.is_busy())
        self.assertEqual(calls, [("first.png", old_key), ("first.png", new_key),
                                 ("second.png", new_key)])
        self.assertFalse(first.exists())
        self.assertFalse(second.exists())
        self.assertEqual(sorted(photo.path.read_bytes() for photo in engine.get_photos()),
                         sorted([first_contents, second_contents]))
        self.assertTrue(engine._auto)
        self.assertEqual(engine._consecutive_failures, 0)
        self.assertFalse(any(kind == "error" or (kind == "auto" and value is False)
                             for kind, value in self.events))

    def test_old_key_failure_does_not_retry_or_overwrite_a_manual_edit(self):
        path = self.photo()
        old_key, new_key = "old-key-placeholder", "new-key-placeholder"
        factory, calls = self._blocked_key_failure(old_key)
        engine = self.engine(analyzer_factory=factory)
        engine.configure("OpenAI", "fake-model", old_key)
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.assertTrue(self.api.entered.wait(2))
        engine.update_photo(str(path), "clothing", "직접입력의상")
        engine.configure("OpenAI", "fake-model", new_key)
        self.api.release.set()
        self.idle(engine)
        photo = engine.get_photos()[0]
        self.assertEqual(calls, [(path.name, old_key)])
        self.assertEqual(photo.analysis["clothing"], ["직접입력의상"])
        self.assertEqual(photo.status, "수동 수정")
        self.assertTrue(photo.manual)
        self.assertFalse(photo.error)
        self.assertEqual(engine._known[path.name]["state"], "processed")
        self.assertFalse(any(kind == "error" for kind, _value in self.events))

    def test_manual_config_change_retry_is_bounded_to_one_per_photo(self):
        self.photo("first.png")
        self.photo("second.png")
        calls = []

        def factory(provider, model, key):
            class Analyzer:
                def analyze(_self, path, cancel):
                    calls.append((path.name, key))
                    next_key = {"first-key-placeholder": "second-key-placeholder",
                                "second-key-placeholder": "third-key-placeholder"}.get(key)
                    if next_key:
                        engine.configure(provider, model, next_key)
                        raise APIError("Old configuration returned HTTP 410", fatal=True)
                    return RESULT

            return Analyzer()

        engine = self.engine(analyzer_factory=factory)
        engine.configure("OpenAI", "fake-model", "first-key-placeholder")
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.idle(engine)
        self.assertEqual(calls, [("first.png", "first-key-placeholder"),
                                 ("first.png", "second-key-placeholder"),
                                 ("second.png", "third-key-placeholder")])
        photos = engine.get_photos()
        self.assertEqual(photos[0].status, "대기")
        self.assertIsNone(photos[0].analysis)
        self.assertEqual(engine._known["first.png"]["state"], "pending")
        self.assertTrue(photos[1].analysis)
        self.assertEqual(engine._consecutive_failures, 0)
        self.assertFalse(any(kind == "error" for kind, _value in self.events))

    def test_fatal_pauses_auto_and_error_does_not_repeat_after_restart(self):
        self.api.failure = APIError("API 권한 오류", fatal=True)
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.set_auto(True)
        self.photo("bad.png")
        wait_until(lambda: any(event == "auto" and value is False for event, value in self.events))
        self.idle(engine)
        self.assertEqual(engine.get_photos()[0].status, "오류")
        engine.shutdown()
        restarted = self.engine()
        restarted.set_auto(True)
        time.sleep(0.2)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.api.calls), 1)
        self.assertEqual(restarted.get_photos()[0].error, "API 권한 오류")

    def test_fatal_manual_batch_leaves_remaining_pending_and_reports_halt(self):
        self.photo("first.png")
        self.photo("second.png")
        self.api.failure = APIError("API 권한 오류", fatal=True)
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.idle(engine)
        self.assertEqual([call[0] for call in self.api.calls], ["first.png"])
        photos = engine.get_photos()
        self.assertEqual(photos[0].status, "오류")
        self.assertEqual(photos[1].status, "대기")
        self.assertIsNone(photos[1].analysis)
        statuses = [value for kind, value in self.events if kind == "status"]
        self.assertEqual(statuses[-1], "오류로 분석을 중지했습니다. API 설정을 확인한 뒤 다시 시작하세요.")
        self.assertNotIn("남은 사진 분석을 마쳤습니다.", statuses)

    def test_explicit_retirement_emits_current_model_before_stopping_batch(self):
        self.photo("first.png")
        self.photo("second.png")
        failure = APIError("Selected model has reached its end of life", fatal=True,
                           status_code=410)
        failure.model_retired = "fake-model"
        self.api.failure = failure
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.configure("NVIDIA", "fake-model", "test-placeholder")
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.idle(engine)
        self.assertEqual([call[0] for call in self.api.calls], ["first.png"])
        retired = [(kind, value) for kind, value in self.events if kind == "model_retired"]
        self.assertEqual(retired, [("model_retired", ("NVIDIA", "fake-model"))])
        retirement_index = self.events.index(retired[0])
        halt_index = next(index for index, (kind, _value) in enumerate(self.events)
                          if kind == "error")
        self.assertLess(retirement_index, halt_index)
        self.assertEqual(engine.get_photos()[1].status, "대기")

    def test_generic_410_and_another_models_retirement_do_not_emit_retirement(self):
        self.photo()
        engine = self.engine()
        for model_retired in (None, "different-model"):
            with self.subTest(model_retired=model_retired):
                failure = APIError("API route returned HTTP 410", fatal=True, status_code=410)
                failure.model_retired = model_retired
                self.api.failure = failure
                engine.start_analysis()
                self.idle(engine)
                self.assertFalse(any(kind == "model_retired" for kind, _value in self.events))
        self.assertEqual(len(self.api.calls), 2)
        self.assertEqual(sum(kind == "error" for kind, _value in self.events), 2)

    def test_old_key_explicit_retirement_does_not_exclude_current_model(self):
        self.photo()
        self.api.release.clear()
        old_key, new_key = "old-key-placeholder", "new-key-placeholder"
        calls = []
        outer = self

        def factory(provider, model, key):
            class Analyzer:
                def analyze(_self, path, cancel):
                    calls.append((path.name, model, key))
                    if key == old_key:
                        outer.api.entered.set()
                        while not outer.api.release.wait(.01):
                            if cancel.is_set():
                                raise Cancelled()
                        failure = APIError("Selected model has reached its end of life",
                                           fatal=True, status_code=410)
                        failure.model_retired = model
                        raise failure
                    return RESULT

            return Analyzer()

        engine = self.engine(analyzer_factory=factory)
        engine.configure("NVIDIA", "fake-model", old_key)
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.assertTrue(self.api.entered.wait(2))
        engine.configure("NVIDIA", "fake-model", new_key)
        self.api.release.set()
        self.idle(engine)
        self.assertEqual([key for _name, _model, key in calls], [old_key, new_key])
        self.assertFalse(any(kind in {"model_retired", "error"} for kind, _value in self.events))
        self.assertEqual(engine.get_photos()[0].status, "분석 완료")

    def test_current_model_retirement_preserves_concurrent_manual_edit_and_halts(self):
        path = self.photo()
        failure = APIError("Selected model has reached its end of life", fatal=True,
                           status_code=410)
        failure.model_retired = "fake-model"
        self.api.failure = failure
        self.api.release.clear()
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.assertTrue(self.api.entered.wait(2))
        engine.update_photo(str(path), "clothing", "직접입력의상")
        self.api.release.set()
        self.idle(engine)
        photo = engine.get_photos()[0]
        self.assertEqual(photo.status, "수동 수정")
        self.assertEqual(photo.analysis["clothing"], ["직접입력의상"])
        self.assertFalse(photo.error)
        self.assertIn(("model_retired", ("OpenAI", "fake-model")), self.events)
        self.assertTrue(any(kind == "error" for kind, _value in self.events))

    def test_three_nonfatal_errors_pause_without_retry_loop(self):
        self.api.failure = APIError("일시 오류")
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.set_auto(True)
        for number in range(4):
            self.photo(f"new{number}.png")
        wait_until(lambda: len(self.api.calls) == 3 and not engine._busy)
        time.sleep(0.15)
        self.assertFalse(engine._auto)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.api.calls), 3)
        self.assertEqual(sum(bool(p.error) for p in engine.get_photos()), 3)

    def test_cancel_stops_worker_and_preserves_pending_for_restart(self):
        engine = self.engine()
        self.api.release.clear()
        engine.set_auto(True)
        self.photo("new.png")
        self.assertTrue(self.api.entered.wait(2))
        engine.shutdown()
        self.assertFalse(engine._worker.is_alive())
        self.assertFalse(engine.get_photos()[0].confirmed)
        record = json.loads((self.root / ".frametag_watch_v2.json").read_text())
        self.assertEqual(record["entries"]["new.png"]["state"], "pending")
        self.api.release.set()
        restarted = self.engine()
        restarted.set_auto(True)
        wait_until(lambda: restarted.get_photos()[0].confirmed)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.api.calls), 2)

    def test_deep_snapshots_and_manual_text_preserved_through_cache(self):
        path = self.photo()
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.update_photo(str(path), "clothing", "내가정한의상, 무늬바지")
        engine.update_photo(str(path), "objects", "의자(바닥), 종이(바닥), 우산")
        external = engine.get_photos()
        external[0].analysis["clothing"].append("외부변경")
        self.assertNotIn("외부변경", engine.get_photos()[0].analysis["clothing"])
        engine.shutdown()
        restarted = self.engine()
        self.assertEqual(restarted.get_photos()[0].analysis["objects"], ["의자(바닥)", "종이(바닥)", "우산"])
        restarted.start_analysis()
        self.idle(restarted)
        self.assertEqual(self.api.calls, [])

    def test_busy_refuses_actual_rename_and_folder_switch(self):
        path = self.photo()
        engine = self.engine()
        self.api.release.clear()
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.assertTrue(self.api.entered.wait(2))
        engine.apply_selected([str(path)])
        engine.undo()
        engine.load_folder(self.root / "missing")
        self.assertTrue(path.exists())
        self.assertEqual(len([value for event, value in self.events if event == "error"]), 3)
        engine.stop_analysis()
        self.idle(engine)

    def test_failed_folder_load_rolls_back_root_and_pauses_auto(self):
        path = self.photo()
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.set_auto(True)
        other = self.root / "other"
        other.mkdir()
        with patch("frametag.engine.recover_pending", side_effect=ValueError("invalid journal")):
            self.assertFalse(engine.load_folder(other))
        self.assertEqual(engine.get_root(), self.root)
        self.assertEqual(engine.get_photos()[0].path, path)
        self.assertFalse(engine._auto)
        self.assertEqual(self.api.calls, [])

    def test_failed_scan_after_new_root_assignment_rolls_back(self):
        path = self.photo()
        engine = self.engine()
        other = self.root / "other"
        other.mkdir()
        with patch.object(engine, "_poll", side_effect=PermissionError("cannot scan")):
            self.assertFalse(engine.load_folder(other))
        self.assertEqual(engine.get_root(), self.root)
        self.assertEqual(engine.get_photos()[0].path, path)

    def test_cached_success_pending_rename_resumes_without_api_charge(self):
        engine = self.engine()
        path = self.photo("arriving.png")
        wait_until(lambda: len(engine.get_photos()) == 1)
        engine.shutdown()
        photo = engine.get_photos()[0]
        photo.analysis = {"gender": "여성", "clothing": ["빨강원피스"], "objects": []}
        photo.proposed_name = "여성_빨강원피스.png"
        photo.status = "분석 완료"
        Cache(self.root).put(photo)
        restarted = self.engine()
        restarted.set_auto(True)
        wait_until(lambda: restarted.get_photos()[0].confirmed)
        self.assertFalse(path.exists())
        self.assertEqual(self.api.calls, [])

    def test_reference_unifies_all_fields_and_select_through_skips_confirmed(self):
        paths = [self.photo(f"photo{number}.png") for number in (1, 2, 10)]
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.idle(engine)
        engine.update_photo(str(paths[0]), "clothing", "직접정한의상")
        engine.update_photo(str(paths[0]), "objects", "의자(바닥), 우산")
        engine.unify_selected([str(p) for p in paths[:2]], str(paths[0]))
        photos = engine.get_photos()
        self.assertEqual(photos[0].analysis, photos[1].analysis)
        self.assertTrue(photos[1].manual)
        engine.apply_selected([str(paths[0])])
        self.assertEqual(engine.select_through(str(paths[2])), [str(paths[1]), str(paths[2])])

    def test_manual_apply_and_undo_do_not_trigger_auto_again(self):
        path = self.photo()
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.idle(engine)
        engine.apply_selected([str(path)])
        self.assertTrue(engine.get_photos()[0].confirmed)
        engine.set_auto(True)
        time.sleep(0.15)
        engine.undo()
        self.assertTrue(path.exists())
        time.sleep(0.15)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.api.calls), 1)
        self.assertFalse(engine.get_photos()[0].confirmed)

    def test_undo_filename_swap_preserves_every_snapshot_and_manifest_entry(self):
        first = self.photo("a.png", color="red")
        second = self.photo("b.png", color="blue")
        before = {path.name: path.read_bytes() for path in (first, second)}
        engine = self.engine()
        # Hold the worker out of the operation to inspect the immediate result, before any rescan.
        with engine._lock:
            engine.update_photo(str(first), "proposed_name", "b.png")
            engine.update_photo(str(second), "proposed_name", "a.png")
            engine.apply_selected([str(first), str(second)])
            engine.undo()
            self.assertEqual({p.path.name for p in engine.get_photos()}, {"a.png", "b.png"})
            self.assertEqual(set(engine._known), {"a.png", "b.png"})
            self.assertEqual({path.name: path.read_bytes() for path in (first, second)}, before)

    def test_mixed_selection_applies_only_ready_photos_and_leaves_pending(self):
        ready = self.photo("ready.png")
        pending = self.photo("pending.png")
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.update_photo(str(ready), "proposed_name", "reviewed.png")
        engine.apply_selected([str(ready), str(pending)])
        photos = {photo.path.name: photo for photo in engine.get_photos()}
        self.assertTrue(photos["reviewed.png"].confirmed)
        self.assertTrue(pending.exists())
        self.assertFalse(photos["pending.png"].confirmed)
        self.assertEqual(photos["pending.png"].status, "대기")
        self.assertEqual(photos["pending.png"].proposed_name, "")
        self.assertEqual(self.api.calls, [])
        self.assertIn(("status", "사진 1장의 파일명을 적용했습니다."), self.events)

    def test_changed_cached_baseline_photo_is_processed(self):
        path = self.photo()
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.idle(engine)
        engine.set_auto(True)
        self.photo("original.png", color="blue")
        wait_until(lambda: engine.get_photos()[0].confirmed)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.api.calls), 2)
        self.assertFalse(path.exists())

    def test_successful_analysis_is_reused_when_auto_rename_failed(self):
        engine = self.engine()
        engine.set_auto(True)
        with patch("frametag.engine.apply_names", side_effect=PermissionError("locked")):
            self.photo("new.png")
            wait_until(lambda: len(engine.get_photos()) == 1 and engine.get_photos()[0].status == "파일명 오류")
        engine.start_analysis()
        self.idle(engine)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.api.calls), 1)
        engine.apply_selected([str(engine.get_photos()[0].path)])
        self.assertTrue(engine.get_photos()[0].confirmed)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

import unittest

from frametag.model_catalog import model_id, model_label, photo_models, restore_model


# Actual NVIDIA catalog supplied by the user on 2026-10-11. Includes every
# image-looking entry and representative text, embedding and safety models.
ACTUAL_CATALOG = [
    "adept/fuyu-8b", "google/deplot", "google/diffusiongemma-26b-a4b-it",
    "google/gemma-3-12b-it", "google/gemma-3-4b-it", "google/gemma-4-31b-it",
    "meta/llama-3.2-11b-vision-instruct", "meta/llama-3.2-90b-vision-instruct",
    "meta/llama-guard-4-12b", "microsoft/kosmos-2",
    "microsoft/phi-3-vision-128k-instruct", "moonshotai/kimi-k2.6", "moonshotai/kimi-k3",
    "nvidia/ai-synthetic-video-detector", "nvidia/cosmos-reason2-8b",
    "nvidia/llama-3.2-nemoretriever-1b-vlm-embed-v1",
    "nvidia/llama-nemotron-embed-vl-1b-v2", "nvidia/nemotron-3-embed-1b",
    "nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning",
    "nvidia/nemotron-3.5-content-safety", "nvidia/nemotron-parse-2.0",
    "nvidia/neva-22b", "nvidia/nvclip", "nvidia/vila", "openai/gpt-oss-20b",
]


class ModelCatalogTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_actual_catalog_offers_supported_generation_candidates(self):
        self.assertEqual(photo_models("NVIDIA", ACTUAL_CATALOG), [
            "moonshotai/kimi-k2.6",
            "nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning",
            "google/gemma-4-31b-it", "google/gemma-3-12b-it", "google/gemma-3-4b-it",
            "meta/llama-3.2-11b-vision-instruct", "meta/llama-3.2-90b-vision-instruct",
        ])

    def test_vision_names_do_not_admit_non_generation_models(self):
        self.assertEqual(photo_models("NVIDIA", [
            "vendor/image-vision-embed", "vendor/vision-retriever", "vendor/vl-reranker",
            "vendor/vision-guard", "vendor/omni-safety", "vendor/vision-parse",
            "vendor/vision-detector", "nvidia/llama-nemotron-embed-vl-1b-v2",
        ]), [])

    def test_new_vision_candidates_still_require_server_membership(self):
        self.assertEqual(photo_models("NVIDIA", ["vendor/new-vl-2", "vendor/new-text"]),
                         ["vendor/new-vl-2"])
        self.assertEqual(photo_models("NVIDIA", []), [])

    def test_exact_omni_selection_roundtrips_and_retired_model_is_cleared(self):
        identifier = "nvidia/nemotron-3-nano-omni-30b-a3b-reasoning"
        self.assertEqual(model_id("NVIDIA", model_label("NVIDIA", identifier)), identifier)
        self.assertEqual(restore_model("NVIDIA", "qwen/qwen3.5-122b-a10b"), "")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

===== BEGIN FILE {"path": "tests/test_rename.py", "role": "current_project", "bytes": 11523, "sha256": "a7271269847e2f4dedad5c71f3742474872eab41607716af0bfffe607f4c4e7d"} =====
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

from frametag.models import Photo
from frametag import rename
from frametag.rename import RenameError, apply_names, recover_pending, undo_last
from frametag.storage import Cache


class RenameTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.root = Path(self.temporary.name)
        self.cache = Cache(self.root)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.temporary.cleanup()

    def photo(self, name, proposed, contents=b"image"):
        path = self.root / name
        path.write_bytes(contents)
        photo = Photo.from_path(path)
        photo.proposed_name = proposed
        photo.manual = True
        self.cache.put(photo)
        return photo

    def test_duplicates_respect_case_and_existing_numbered_names(self):
        (self.root / "PERSON.JPG").write_bytes(b"existing")
        (self.root / "person_2.jpg").write_bytes(b"existing 2")
        a = self.photo("a.jpg", "person.jpg", b"A")
        b = self.photo("b.jpg", "person.jpg", b"B")
        result = apply_names(self.root, [a, b], self.cache)
        self.assertEqual([new.name for old, new in result], ["person_3.jpg", "person_4.jpg"])
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "PERSON.JPG").read_bytes(), b"existing")
        self.assertEqual(a.path.read_bytes(), b"A")
        self.assertEqual(b.path.read_bytes(), b"B")
        self.assertTrue(self.cache.get(a.path).manual)
        self.assertTrue(self.cache.get(a.path).confirmed)
        self.assertIsNone(self.cache.get(self.root / "a.jpg"))

    def test_filename_swap_preserves_both_files_and_cache_then_undo(self):
        a = self.photo("a.jpg", "b.jpg", b"A")
        b = self.photo("b.jpg", "a.jpg", b"B")
        apply_names(self.root, [a, b], self.cache)
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "a.jpg").read_bytes(), b"B")
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "b.jpg").read_bytes(), b"A")
        self.assertEqual(self.cache.get(self.root / "a.jpg").proposed_name, "a.jpg")
        result = undo_last(self.root, self.cache)
        self.assertEqual([(old.name, new.name) for old, new in result], [("b.jpg", "a.jpg"), ("a.jpg", "b.jpg")])
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "a.jpg").read_bytes(), b"A")
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "b.jpg").read_bytes(), b"B")
        self.assertFalse(self.cache.get(self.root / "a.jpg").confirmed)
        self.assertEqual(undo_last(self.root, self.cache), [])

    def test_stale_analysis_and_invalid_manual_name_are_rejected(self):
        photo = self.photo("a.jpg", "person.jpg")
        photo.path.write_bytes(b"different contents")
        with self.assertRaises(RenameError):
            apply_names(self.root, [photo], self.cache)
        photo = Photo.from_path(photo.path)
        photo.proposed_name = "../escape.jpg"
        with self.assertRaises(RenameError):
            apply_names(self.root, [photo], self.cache)
        self.assertTrue((self.root / "a.jpg").exists())

    def test_ordinary_second_move_failure_rolls_back_without_history(self):
        a = self.photo("a.jpg", "new-a.jpg", b"A")
        b = self.photo("b.jpg", "new-b.jpg", b"B")
        original_move = rename._move_no_clobber
        failed = False

        def fail_once(source, target, entry):
            nonlocal failed
            if target.name == "new-b.jpg" and not failed:
                failed = True
                raise OSError("simulated locked file")
            return original_move(source, target, entry)

        with patch.object(rename, "_move_no_clobber", side_effect=fail_once):
            with self.assertRaises(RenameError):
                apply_names(self.root, [a, b], self.cache)
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "a.jpg").read_bytes(), b"A")
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "b.jpg").read_bytes(), b"B")
        self.assertFalse((self.root / "new-a.jpg").exists())
        self.assertFalse((self.root / rename._PENDING).exists())
        self.assertEqual(undo_last(self.root, self.cache), [])
        self.assertFalse(a.confirmed)

    def test_new_external_destination_is_preserved_and_operation_rolls_back(self):
        photo = self.photo("a.jpg", "new.jpg", b"ORIGINAL")
        original_move = rename._move_no_clobber

        def competing_file(source, target, entry):
            if target.name == "new.jpg":
                target.write_bytes(b"EXTERNAL")
            return original_move(source, target, entry)

        with patch.object(rename, "_move_no_clobber", side_effect=competing_file):
            with self.assertRaises(RenameError):
                apply_names(self.root, [photo], self.cache)
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "a.jpg").read_bytes(), b"ORIGINAL")
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "new.jpg").read_bytes(), b"EXTERNAL")

    def test_crash_after_first_stage_is_recovered_on_reopen(self):
        photo = self.photo("a.jpg", "new.jpg", b"ORIGINAL")
        original_move = rename._move_no_clobber

        def crash_after_stage(source, target, entry):
            original_move(source, target, entry)
            if target.name.endswith(".tmp"):
                raise KeyboardInterrupt("simulate process termination")

        with patch.object(rename, "_move_no_clobber", side_effect=crash_after_stage):
            with self.assertRaises(KeyboardInterrupt):
                apply_names(self.root, [photo], self.cache)
        self.assertFalse((self.root / "a.jpg").exists())
        self.assertTrue((self.root / rename._PENDING).exists())
        recover_pending(self.root, Cache(self.root))
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "a.jpg").read_bytes(), b"ORIGINAL")
        self.assertFalse((self.root / rename._PENDING).exists())

    def test_crash_mid_swap_restores_both_original_names(self):
        self.photo("a.jpg", "b.jpg", b"A")
        a = self.cache.get(self.root / "a.jpg")
        b = self.photo("b.jpg", "a.jpg", b"B")
        original_move = rename._move_no_clobber

        def crash_mid_commit(source, target, entry):
            original_move(source, target, entry)
            if target.name == "b.jpg":
                raise KeyboardInterrupt("simulate process termination during swap")

        with patch.object(rename, "_move_no_clobber", side_effect=crash_mid_commit):
            with self.assertRaises(KeyboardInterrupt):
                apply_names(self.root, [a, b], self.cache)
        recover_pending(self.root, self.cache)
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "a.jpg").read_bytes(), b"A")
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "b.jpg").read_bytes(), b"B")
        self.assertFalse((self.root / rename._PENDING).exists())

    def test_recovery_preserves_external_file_at_original_name(self):
        photo = self.photo("a.jpg", "new.jpg", b"ORIGINAL")
        original_move = rename._move_no_clobber

        def crash_after_stage(source, target, entry):
            original_move(source, target, entry)
            raise KeyboardInterrupt("simulate crash")

        with patch.object(rename, "_move_no_clobber", side_effect=crash_after_stage):
            with self.assertRaises(KeyboardInterrupt):
                apply_names(self.root, [photo], self.cache)
        (self.root / "a.jpg").write_bytes(b"EXTERNAL")
        with self.assertRaises(RenameError):
            recover_pending(self.root, self.cache)
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "a.jpg").read_bytes(), b"EXTERNAL")
        self.assertTrue((self.root / rename._PENDING).exists())
        self.assertEqual(next(self.root.glob(".frametag-*.tmp")).read_bytes(), b"ORIGINAL")
        (self.root / "a.jpg").unlink()
        recover_pending(self.root, self.cache)
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "a.jpg").read_bytes(), b"ORIGINAL")

    def test_metadata_failure_reports_incomplete_and_remains_recoverable(self):
        photo = self.photo("a.jpg", "new.jpg", b"ORIGINAL")
        with patch.object(self.cache, "relocate_many", side_effect=OSError("simulated database failure")):
            with self.assertRaises(RenameError):
                apply_names(self.root, [photo], self.cache)
        self.assertFalse(photo.confirmed)
        self.assertTrue((self.root / "new.jpg").exists())
        self.assertTrue((self.root / rename._PENDING).exists())
        recover_pending(self.root, self.cache)
        self.assertTrue(self.cache.get(self.root / "new.jpg").confirmed)
        undo_last(self.root, self.cache)
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "a.jpg").read_bytes(), b"ORIGINAL")

    def test_committed_files_finish_metadata_after_restart(self):
        photo = self.photo("a.jpg", "new.jpg", b"ORIGINAL")
        with patch.object(rename, "_finish", side_effect=KeyboardInterrupt("simulate crash")):
            with self.assertRaises(KeyboardInterrupt):
                apply_names(self.root, [photo], self.cache)
        self.assertTrue((self.root / "new.jpg").exists())
        recover_pending(self.root, Cache(self.root))
        self.assertTrue(self.cache.get(self.root / "new.jpg").confirmed)
        self.assertFalse((self.root / rename._PENDING).exists())
        undo_last(self.root, self.cache)
        self.assertEqual((self.root / "a.jpg").read_bytes(), b"ORIGINAL")

    def test_undo_refuses_external_original_and_modified_renamed_file(self):
        photo = self.photo("a.jpg", "new.jpg", b"ORIGINAL")
        apply_names(self.root, [photo], self.cache)
        (self.root / "A.JPG").write_bytes(b"EXTERNAL")
        with self.assertRaises(RenameError):
            undo_last(self.root, self.cache)
        (self.root / "A.JPG").unlink()
        photo.path.write_bytes(b"EDITED")
        with self.assertRaises(RenameError):
            undo_last(self.root, self.cache)
        self.assertEqual(photo.path.read_bytes(), b"EDITED")

    def test_long_unicode_duplicate_names_fit_filename_limit(self):
        proposed = "가" * 78 + ".jpg"  # 238 bytes before adding a duplicate suffix.
        a = self.photo("a.jpg", proposed, b"A")
        b = self.photo("b.jpg", proposed, b"B")
        apply_names(self.root, [a, b], self.cache)
        self.assertNotEqual(a.path, b.path)
        self.assertLessEqual(len(b.path.name.encode("utf-8")), 240)
        self.assertTrue(b.path.stem.endswith("_2"))

    def test_no_op_name_still_persists_confirmation(self):
        photo = self.photo("a.jpg", "a.jpg")
        self.assertEqual(apply_names(self.root, [photo], self.cache), [])
        self.assertTrue(photo.confirmed)
        self.assertTrue(self.cache.get(photo.path).confirmed)
        self.assertEqual(undo_last(self.root, self.cache), [])

    def test_case_only_rename_and_undo_preserves_exact_capitalization(self):
        photo = self.photo("photo.jpg", "PHOTO.jpg")
        apply_names(self.root, [photo], self.cache)
        self.assertIn("PHOTO.jpg", [path.name for path in self.root.iterdir()])
        undo_last(self.root, self.cache)
        self.assertIn("photo.jpg", [path.name for path in self.root.iterdir()])

    def test_selected_hardlinks_are_rejected_before_any_changes(self):
        a = self.photo("a.jpg", "new-a.jpg")
        os.link(a.path, self.root / "b.jpg")
        b = Photo.from_path(self.root / "b.jpg")
        b.proposed_name = "new-b.jpg"
        with self.assertRaises(RenameError):
            apply_names(self.root, [a, b], self.cache)
        self.assertTrue(a.path.exists())
        self.assertTrue(b.path.exists())
        self.assertFalse((self.root / rename._PENDING).exists())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

import concurrent.futures
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tempfile
import types
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

from frametag.models import Photo
from frametag.storage import Cache, KeyStore, KeyStoreError, SettingsStore


class StorageTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.root = Path(self.temporary.name)

    def tearDown(self):
        self.temporary.cleanup()

    def photo(self, name="one.jpg"):
        path = self.root / name
        path.write_bytes(b"photo contents")
        return Photo.from_path(path)

    def test_cache_persists_manual_confirmation_without_touching_legacy(self):
        legacy = self.root / ".frametag_cache.sqlite"
        legacy.write_bytes(b"unknown legacy data")
        photo = self.photo()
        photo.analysis = {"gender": "여성"}
        photo.proposed_name = "직접 선택.jpg"
        photo.manual = photo.confirmed = True
        photo.model = "test-model"
        photo.status = "확인 완료"
        photo.error = "previous diagnostic"
        photo.revision = 3
        Cache(self.root).put(photo)
        loaded = Cache(self.root).get(photo.path)
        self.assertEqual(loaded, photo)
        self.assertEqual(legacy.read_bytes(), b"unknown legacy data")

    def test_changed_content_invalidates_manual_results(self):
        photo = self.photo()
        photo.manual = photo.confirmed = True
        cache = Cache(self.root)
        cache.put(photo)
        photo.path.write_bytes(b"other contents")
        os.utime(photo.path, ns=(photo.mtime_ns + 1, photo.mtime_ns + 1))
        self.assertIsNone(cache.get(photo.path))

    def test_cache_supports_worker_threads(self):
        photos = [self.photo(f"{index}.jpg") for index in range(12)]
        cache = Cache(self.root)
        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as executor:
            list(executor.map(cache.put, photos))
            loaded = list(executor.map(lambda photo: cache.get(photo.path), photos))
        self.assertEqual(loaded, photos)

    def test_bulk_cache_read_uses_one_connection_and_rejects_changed_fingerprints(self):
        photos = [self.photo(f"{index}.jpg") for index in range(20)]
        photos[0].manual, photos[0].revision = True, 5
        cache = Cache(self.root)
        cache.put_many(photos)
        paths = {photo.path: (photo.size, photo.mtime_ns) for photo in photos}
        paths[photos[-1].path] = (photos[-1].size + 1, photos[-1].mtime_ns)
        with patch.object(cache, "_connect", wraps=cache._connect) as connect:
            loaded = cache.get_many(paths)
        self.assertEqual(connect.call_count, 1)
        self.assertEqual(loaded, {str(photo.path): photo for photo in photos[:-1]})
        self.assertEqual(cache.get_many({}), {})

    def test_settings_are_persistent_and_recursively_exclude_secrets(self):
        store = SettingsStore(self.root / "config")
        store.save({"folder": "D:/사진", "provider": "OpenAI", "api_key": "test-only-key",
                    "nested": {"NVIDIA_API_KEY": "another-test-key", "model": "vision"},
                    "items": [{"token": "token-value", "value": 4}]})
        self.assertEqual(store.load(), {"folder": "D:/사진", "provider": "OpenAI",
                                      "nested": {"model": "vision"}, "items": [{"value": 4}]})
        self.assertNotIn("test-only-key", store.path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        store.save({"folder": "D:/새폴더"})
        self.assertEqual(SettingsStore(store.directory).load(), {"folder": "D:/새폴더"})
        self.assertEqual(list(store.directory.glob("*.tmp")), [])

    def test_corrupted_settings_are_preserved(self):
        store = SettingsStore(self.root)
        store.path.write_text("unfinished json", encoding="utf-8")
        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
            store.load()
        self.assertEqual(store.path.read_text(), "unfinished json")

    def test_keystore_uses_secure_backend_and_normalizes_provider(self):
        values = {}
        backend = types.SimpleNamespace(
            get_password=lambda service, provider: values.get((service, provider)),
            set_password=lambda service, provider, value: values.__setitem__((service, provider), value),
            delete_password=lambda service, provider: values.pop((service, provider)),
        )
        store = KeyStore()
        with patch.object(KeyStore, "_backend", return_value=backend):
            self.assertEqual(store.get("OpenAI"), "")
            store.set("OpenAI", "fake-test-key")
            self.assertEqual(store.get("openai"), "fake-test-key")
            store.delete("OPENAI")
            self.assertEqual(store.get("OpenAI"), "")
            store.delete("OpenAI")

    def test_plaintext_keyring_backend_is_rejected(self):
        backend_type = type("PlaintextKeyring", (), {"__module__": "keyrings.alt.file", "priority": 1})
        fake = types.SimpleNamespace(get_keyring=lambda: backend_type())
        with patch.dict(sys.modules, {"keyring": fake}):
            with self.assertRaises(KeyStoreError):
                KeyStore().set("OpenAI", "fake-test-key")
        self.assertEqual(list(self.root.iterdir()), [])

    def test_windows_loads_credential_manager_without_entrypoint_discovery(self):
        values = {}
        backend_type = type("WinVaultKeyring", (), {
            "priority": 5,
            "get_password": lambda self, service, provider: values.get((service, provider)),
            "set_password": lambda self, service, provider, value: values.__setitem__((service, provider), value),
            "delete_password": lambda self, service, provider: values.pop((service, provider)),
        })
        keyring_module = types.ModuleType("keyring")
        keyring_module.__path__ = []
        keyring_module.get_keyring = lambda: self.fail("Windows must not depend on entry-point discovery")
        backends_module = types.ModuleType("keyring.backends")
        backends_module.__path__ = []
        windows_module = types.ModuleType("keyring.backends.Windows")
        windows_module.WinVaultKeyring = backend_type
        modules = {"keyring": keyring_module, "keyring.backends": backends_module,
                   "keyring.backends.Windows": windows_module}
        with patch.dict(sys.modules, modules), patch.object(os, "name", "nt"):
            store = KeyStore()
            store.set("NVIDIA", "fake-test-key")
            self.assertEqual(store.get("nvidia"), "fake-test-key")
            store.delete("NVIDIA")
            self.assertEqual(store.get("NVIDIA"), "")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

"""Desktop integration checks; skipped when no display is available."""
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import threading
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

from PIL import Image

from frametag.api import APIError, Cancelled, ModelDiscovery
from frametag.engine import Engine
from frametag.models import Photo
from frametag.model_catalog import NVIDIA_DISCOVERABLE, NVIDIA_UNVERIFIED_LABEL, model_label
from frametag.storage import Cache, SettingsStore
from frametag.ui import FrameTagApp


class FakeKeys:
    def __init__(self):
        self.keys = {}

    def get(self, provider):
        return self.keys.get(provider, "")

    def set(self, provider, key):
        self.keys[provider] = key

    def delete(self, provider):
        self.keys.pop(provider, None)


class FakeAnalyzer:
    calls = []

    def __init__(self, provider, model, key):
        pass

    def analyze(self, path, cancel):
        self.calls.append(path.name)
        return {"gender": "불명", "clothing": ["흰색반팔"], "objects": []}


@unittest.skipUnless(os.environ.get("DISPLAY") or os.name == "nt", "A desktop display is required")
class UITests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temporary.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temporary.name)
        self.folder = self.root / "photos"
        self.folder.mkdir()
        for number in range(1, 4):
            Image.new("RGB", (40, 60), (number * 50, 90, 130)).save(self.folder / f"photo{number}.jpg")
        self.settings = SettingsStore(self.root / "config")
        self.keys = FakeKeys()
        FakeAnalyzer.calls = []
        self.errors = []
        environment = patch.dict(os.environ, {"NVIDIA_API_KEY": "", "OPENAI_API_KEY": ""})
        environment.start()
        self.addCleanup(environment.stop)
        for target, arguments in (
            ("frametag.ui.FrameTagApp._setup_tray", {"return_value": None}),
            ("frametag.ui.messagebox.showerror", {"side_effect": lambda *a, **kw: self.errors.append(a)}),
            ("frametag.ui.messagebox.showinfo", {"return_value": None}),
            ("frametag.ui.messagebox.askyesno", {"return_value": True}),
        ):
            mock = patch(target, **arguments)
            mock.start()
            self.addCleanup(mock.stop)
        self.apps = []
        self.addCleanup(self._close_apps)
        self.app = self._new_app()
        # Let the automatic startup callback finish before setting the test folder.
        deadline = time.monotonic() + .25
        self.wait(lambda: time.monotonic() >= deadline)
        self.app.folder.set(str(self.folder))
        self.app.api_key.set("mock-nvidia-key-not-valid")
        self._verify_model_for_test()
        self.app._load_folder()
        self.wait(lambda: len(self.app.photos) == 3 and not self.app._loading)

    def _new_app(self):
        app = FrameTagApp(settings_store=self.settings, key_store=self.keys,
                         engine_factory=lambda callback: Engine(
                             callback, interval=.03, stable_scans=2, analyzer_factory=FakeAnalyzer))
        self.apps.append(app)
        return app

    def _close_apps(self):
        for app in self.apps:
            if not app._closed:
                app._exit()

    def _verify_model_for_test(self, model="test-vision-model"):
        self.app.model.set(model)
        self.app._verified_models[("NVIDIA", self.app.api_key.get().strip())] = {model}

    def wait(self, predicate, timeout=4):
        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            self.app.update()
            if predicate():
                return
            time.sleep(.01)
        self.fail(f"GUI wait timed out: {self.app.status.get()}")

    def test_review_selection_apply_and_undo_preserve_photo_bytes(self):
        hashes = sorted(hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for path in self.folder.glob("*.jpg"))
        self.app._analyze()
        self.wait(lambda: all(photo.analysis for photo in self.app.photos) and not self.app._busy)
        first, second, third = [str(photo.path) for photo in self.app.photos]
        self.app.engine.update_photo(first, "objects", "우산, 종이(바닥)")
        self.wait(lambda: self.app.by_id[first].manual)
        self.app.tree.selection_set(first, second)
        self.app._set_reference(first)
        self.app._unify()
        self.wait(lambda: self.app.by_id[second].manual)
        self.app.tree.focus(second)
        self.app._select_through()
        self.assertEqual(set(self.app.tree.selection()), {first, second})
        self.app._apply()
        self.wait(lambda: sum(photo.confirmed for photo in self.app.photos) == 2)
        self.app.tree.focus(third)
        self.app._select_through()
        self.assertEqual(self.app.tree.selection(), (third,))
        self.app._undo()
        self.wait(lambda: not any(photo.confirmed for photo in self.app.photos))
        self.assertEqual(sorted(path.name for path in self.folder.glob("*.jpg")),
                         ["photo1.jpg", "photo2.jpg", "photo3.jpg"])
        self.assertEqual(sorted(hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for path in self.folder.glob("*.jpg")), hashes)
        self.app.search.set("우산 종이")
        self.wait(lambda: self.app.count.get() == "2 / 3")
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_folder_provider_and_keys_restore_without_plaintext_settings(self):
        self.app._save_settings()
        self.app.provider.set("OpenAI")
        self.app._provider_changed()
        self.assertEqual(self.app.api_key.get(), "")
        self.app.api_key.set("mock-openai-key-not-valid")
        self.app.model.set("mock-vision-model")
        self.app._save_settings()
        self.app._exit()
        self.app = self._new_app()
        self.assertEqual(self.app.provider.get(), "OpenAI")
        self.assertEqual(self.app.model.get(), "mock-vision-model")
        self.assertEqual(self.app.api_key.get(), "mock-openai-key-not-valid")
        self.assertEqual(self.app.folder.get(), str(self.folder))
        self.wait(lambda: len(self.app.photos) == 3 and not self.app._loading)
        self.app.provider.set("NVIDIA")
        self.app._provider_changed()
        self.assertEqual(self.app.api_key.get(), "mock-nvidia-key-not-valid")
        settings_text = self.settings.path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(settings_text)["folder"], str(self.folder))
        self.assertNotIn("mock-nvidia-key-not-valid", settings_text)
        self.assertNotIn("mock-openai-key-not-valid", settings_text)
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_new_key_is_used_when_analysis_starts_before_save_debounce(self):
        new_key = "mock-replacement-nvidia-key-not-valid"
        captured = []

        def factory(provider, model, key):
            captured.append((provider, model, key))
            return FakeAnalyzer(provider, model, key)

        with patch.object(self.app.engine, "_factory", side_effect=factory):
            self.app.api_key.set(new_key)
            self._verify_model_for_test()
            self.app._analyze()
            self.wait(lambda: all(photo.analysis for photo in self.app.photos) and not self.app._busy)
        self.assertEqual(len(captured), 3)
        self.assertTrue(all(provider == "NVIDIA" and key == new_key
                            for provider, _model, key in captured))
        self.assertEqual(self.keys.get("NVIDIA"), new_key)
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_model_discovery_uses_entered_key_before_save_debounce(self):
        new_key = "mock-discovery-nvidia-key-not-valid"
        available = list(NVIDIA_DISCOVERABLE)[:3]
        result = ModelDiscovery(available, [], available, [])
        preferred = self.app.model.get()
        with patch("frametag.ui.discover_photo_models", return_value=result) as lookup:
            self.app.api_key.set(new_key)
            self.app._fetch_models()
            self.wait(lambda: lookup.called and not self.app._models_pending)
        self.assertEqual(lookup.call_count, 1)
        self.assertEqual(lookup.call_args.args[:2], ("NVIDIA", new_key))
        self.assertEqual(lookup.call_args.args[2], preferred)
        self.assertIsInstance(lookup.call_args.args[3], threading.Event)
        self.assertTrue(callable(lookup.call_args.kwargs["progress"]))
        self.assertIn("excluded", lookup.call_args.kwargs)
        self.assertEqual(self.app._verified_models[("NVIDIA", new_key)], set(available))
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_failed_key_persistence_still_updates_current_session_key(self):
        old_key = self.app.api_key.get()
        self.wait(lambda: self.app.engine._config[2] == old_key)
        # Exercise the key-only debounce, without an unrelated settings save
        # also configuring the engine and concealing a persistence failure.
        if self.app._save_id:
            self.app.after_cancel(self.app._save_id)
            self.app._save_id = None
        new_key = "mock-unsaved-nvidia-key-not-valid"
        with patch.object(self.keys, "set", side_effect=RuntimeError("Credential store unavailable")) as save:
            self.app.api_key.set(new_key)
            self.wait(lambda: save.called and self.app.engine._config[2] == new_key)
        save.assert_called_once_with("NVIDIA", new_key)
        self.assertEqual(self.keys.get("NVIDIA"), old_key)
        self.assertTrue(self.app._key_dirty)
        self.assertNotIn(new_key, self.settings.path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_retired_model_restores_blank_and_verified_choices_save_actual_api_id(self):
        self.app._save_settings()
        self.app._exit()
        saved = self.settings.load()
        saved["models"]["NVIDIA"] = "qwen/qwen3.5-122b-a10b"
        self.settings.save(saved)
        self.app = self._new_app()
        self.assertEqual(self.app.model.get(), "")
        self.assertEqual(self.app.model_choice.get(), NVIDIA_UNVERIFIED_LABEL)
        self.wait(lambda: len(self.app.photos) == 3 and not self.app._loading)
        available = list(NVIDIA_DISCOVERABLE)[:3]
        result = ModelDiscovery(available, [], available, [])
        with patch("frametag.ui.discover_photo_models", return_value=result):
            self.app._fetch_models()
            self.wait(lambda: self.app.model.get() == available[0])
        self.assertEqual(list(self.app.model_combo["values"]), [model_label("NVIDIA", name) for name in available])
        self.app.model_choice.set(model_label("NVIDIA", available[1]))
        self.app._save_settings()
        self.assertEqual(self.settings.load()["models"]["NVIDIA"], available[1])
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_nvidia_analysis_and_auto_watch_require_current_key_verification(self):
        self.app._verified_models.clear()

                    self.app.model.set(selected)

                    self.app._toggle_auto()

            self.assertGreaterEqual(lookup.call_count, 1)
        self.assertFalse(self.app.engine.is_busy())

        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_partial_model_verification_report_retains_later_failures_and_redacts_key(self):
        available = list(NVIDIA_DISCOVERABLE)[:3]
        key = self.app.api_key.get()
        notes = ["첫 번째 후보의 이미지 입력을 확인하지 못했습니다.",
                 "두 번째 후보의 테스트 색상 확인이 실패했습니다.",
                 f"마지막 후보 오류: unsupported image input; credential={key}"]
        result = ModelDiscovery([available[0]], [], available, notes)
        with patch("frametag.ui.discover_photo_models", return_value=result) as discovery:
            self.app._fetch_models()
            self.wait(lambda: not self.app._models_pending)
        dialog = next(widget for widget in self.app.winfo_children()
                      if widget.winfo_class() == "Toplevel"
                      and widget.title() == "FrameTag 사진 모델 확인 결과")
        self.assertFalse(dialog.grab_current())
        def descendants(widget):
            for child in widget.winfo_children():
                yield child
                yield from descendants(child)
        text = next(widget for widget in descendants(dialog) if widget.winfo_class() == "Text")
        report = text.get("1.0", "end-1c")
        self.assertIn("공급자: NVIDIA", report)
        self.assertIn(available[0], report)
        self.assertIn(notes[0], report)
        self.assertIn(notes[1], report)
        self.assertIn("마지막 후보 오류: unsupported image input", report)
        self.assertIn("[API 키 숨김]", report)
        self.assertNotIn(key, report)
        self.assertNotIn(str(self.folder), report)
        self.assertNotIn("photo1.jpg", report)
        self.assertNotIn("마지막 후보 오류", self.app.status.get())
        copy_button = next(widget for widget in descendants(dialog)
                           if widget.winfo_class() == "TButton"
                           and widget.cget("text") == "모델 확인 결과 복사")
        copy_button.invoke()
        self.assertEqual(self.app.clipboard_get(), report)
        self.assertEqual(self.app.model.get(), available[0])
        self.assertEqual(list(self.app.model_combo["values"]), [model_label("NVIDIA", available[0])])
        self.assertTrue(self.app._ready())
        self.assertEqual(discovery.call_count, 1)
        self.assertEqual(FakeAnalyzer.calls, [])
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)
        dialog.destroy()

    def test_empty_model_verification_opens_copyable_report_without_modal_error(self):
        result = ModelDiscovery([], [], list(NVIDIA_DISCOVERABLE)[:2], [])

        self.assertEqual(list(self.app.model_combo["values"]), [])

        self.assertEqual(FakeAnalyzer.calls, [])
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_lookup_error_connection_failure_report_has_model_path_and_no_key(self):
        selected, key = self.app.model.get(), self.app.api_key.get()
        endpoint = "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1/models"
        message = ("API 연결에 실패했습니다. 인터넷 연결과 서버 상태를 확인하세요.\n"
                   f"요청: GET {endpoint}\n연결 오류 종류: ConnectError; credential={key}")
        with patch("frametag.ui.discover_photo_models", side_effect=APIError(message)) as lookup, \
                patch.object(self.app, "_show_diagnostic") as show:
            self.app._fetch_models()
            self.wait(lambda: not self.app._models_pending)
        self.assertEqual(show.call_count, 1)
        report = show.call_args.args[0]
        self.assertIn("통과한 모델이 없습니다.", report)
        self.assertIn(selected, report)
        self.assertIn(endpoint, report)
        self.assertIn("ConnectError", report)
        self.assertNotIn(key, report)
        self.assertNotIn(str(self.folder), report)
        self.assertNotIn("photo1.jpg", report)
        self.assertEqual(show.call_args.kwargs["copy_label"], "모델 확인 결과 복사")
        self.assertEqual(lookup.call_count, 1)
        self.assertFalse(self.app._verified_models.get(("NVIDIA", key)))
        with patch.object(self.app, "_fetch_models"):
            self.assertFalse(self.app._ready())
        self.assertFalse(self.app.auto_watch.get())
        self.assertFalse(self.app.engine._auto)
        self.assertEqual(FakeAnalyzer.calls, [])
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_lookup_error_after_key_change_does_not_open_a_report(self):
        entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
        self.addCleanup(release.set)

        def lookup(*args, **kwargs):
            entered.set()
            release.wait(2)
            raise APIError("이전 키의 API 연결에 실패했습니다.")

        with patch("frametag.ui.discover_photo_models", side_effect=lookup), \
                patch.object(self.app, "_show_model_verification") as show:
            self.app._fetch_models()
            self.wait(entered.is_set)
            self.app.api_key.set("mock-changed-before-lookup-error")
            release.set()
            self.wait(lambda: not self.app._models_pending)
            show.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual(FakeAnalyzer.calls, [])
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_lookup_error_cancelled_before_queue_poll_does_not_open_a_report(self):
        with patch("frametag.ui.discover_photo_models", side_effect=APIError("API 연결에 실패했습니다.")), \
                patch.object(self.app, "_show_model_verification") as show:
            self.app._fetch_models()
            deadline = time.monotonic() + 1
            while True:
                with self.app.events.mutex:
                    queued = any(name == "models_error" for name, _ in self.app.events.queue)
                if queued or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    break
                time.sleep(.005)
            self.assertTrue(queued)
            self.app._fetch_models()
            self.wait(lambda: not self.app._models_pending)
            show.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual(str(self.app.models_button["state"]), "normal")
        self.assertEqual(FakeAnalyzer.calls, [])
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_startup_auto_watch_checks_saved_key_before_enable_and_new_photo_analysis(self):
        saved_key = self.app.api_key.get()
        self.app.auto_watch.set(True)
        self.app._save_settings()
        self.app._exit()
        entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
        self.addCleanup(release.set)
        available = ["test-vision-model"]

        def lookup(provider, key, preferred, cancel, **kwargs):
            entered.set()
            release.wait(3)
            return ModelDiscovery(available, [], available, [])

        with patch("frametag.ui.discover_photo_models", side_effect=lookup) as discovery:
            self.app = self._new_app()
            self.wait(entered.is_set)
            self.assertFalse(self.app.auto_watch.get())
            self.assertFalse(self.app.engine._auto)
            self.assertEqual(FakeAnalyzer.calls, [])
            self.assertEqual(discovery.call_args.args[:2], ("NVIDIA", saved_key))
            release.set()
            self.wait(lambda: self.app.auto_watch.get() and self.app.engine._auto)
        incoming = self.folder / "incoming.jpg"
        Image.new("RGB", (45, 65), "white").save(incoming)
        self.wait(lambda: sum(photo.confirmed for photo in self.app.photos) == 1
                  and not self.app._busy)
        self.assertEqual(FakeAnalyzer.calls, ["incoming.jpg"])
        self.assertTrue(all((self.folder / f"photo{number}.jpg").exists()
                            for number in range(1, 4)))
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_startup_does_not_enable_auto_with_a_different_verified_model(self):
        self.app.auto_watch.set(True)
        self.app._save_settings()
        self.app._exit()
        replacement = next(iter(NVIDIA_DISCOVERABLE))
        result = ModelDiscovery([replacement], [], [replacement], [])
        with patch("frametag.ui.discover_photo_models", return_value=result) as discovery:
            self.app = self._new_app()
            self.wait(lambda: discovery.called and not self.app._models_pending
                      and self.app.model.get() == replacement)
        self.assertFalse(self.app.auto_watch.get())
        self.assertFalse(self.app.engine._auto)
        self.assertEqual(FakeAnalyzer.calls, [])
        self.assertTrue(all((self.folder / f"photo{number}.jpg").exists()
                            for number in range(1, 4)))
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_server_retired_model_persists_and_is_not_offered_after_restart(self):
        available, retired = list(NVIDIA_DISCOVERABLE)[:2]
        self._verify_model_for_test(retired)
        result = ModelDiscovery([available], [retired], [available, retired], [])
        with patch("frametag.ui.discover_photo_models", return_value=result):
            self.app._fetch_models()
            self.wait(lambda: not self.app._models_pending and self.app.model.get() == available)
        self.app._save_settings()
        self.assertIn(retired, self.settings.load()["retired_nvidia_models"])
        self.app._exit()
        # A stale preference must not resurrect a model the server has retired.
        saved = self.settings.load()
        saved["models"]["NVIDIA"] = retired
        self.settings.save(saved)
        self.app = self._new_app()
        self.assertEqual(self.app.model.get(), "")
        self.assertEqual(self.app.model_choice.get(), NVIDIA_UNVERIFIED_LABEL)
        self.wait(lambda: len(self.app.photos) == 3 and not self.app._loading)
        with patch("frametag.ui.discover_photo_models", return_value=result) as discovery:
            self.app._fetch_models()
            self.wait(lambda: not self.app._models_pending and self.app.model.get() == available)
        self.assertIn(retired, discovery.call_args.kwargs["excluded"])
        self.assertNotIn(model_label("NVIDIA", retired), self.app.model_combo["values"])
        self.assertNotIn(retired, self.app._verified_models[("NVIDIA", self.app.api_key.get())])
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_model_verification_animates_and_can_be_cancelled_without_blocking_gui(self):
        entered = threading.Event()

        def lookup(provider, key, preferred, cancel, **kwargs):
            entered.set()
            if not cancel.wait(3):
                raise AssertionError("Model verification was not cancelled")
            raise Cancelled()

        with patch("frametag.ui.discover_photo_models", side_effect=lookup):
            started = time.monotonic()
            self.app._fetch_models()
            self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - started, .25)
            self.wait(entered.is_set)
            self.assertTrue(self.app._models_pending)
            self.assertIn("모델 확인 중", self.app.loading_text.get())
            self.assertEqual(str(self.app.progress["mode"]), "indeterminate")
            self.assertEqual(self.app.models_button["text"], "모델 확인 중지")
            initial_progress = float(self.app.progress["value"])
            heartbeat = []
            self.app.after(30, lambda: heartbeat.append(True))
            self.wait(lambda: bool(heartbeat), timeout=.5)
            self.wait(lambda: float(self.app.progress["value"]) > initial_progress, timeout=.5)
            started = time.monotonic()
            self.app.models_button.invoke()
            self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - started, .25)
            self.assertTrue(self.app._model_cancel.is_set())
            self.assertEqual(str(self.app.models_button["state"]), "disabled")
            self.wait(lambda: not self.app._models_pending)
        self.assertEqual(str(self.app.models_button["state"]), "normal")
        self.assertEqual(str(self.app.progress["mode"]), "determinate")
        self.assertEqual(self.app.loading_text.get(), "")
        self.assertEqual(FakeAnalyzer.calls, [])
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_cancelled_lookup_does_not_apply_a_result_already_waiting_in_queue(self):
        original = self.app.model.get()
        replacement = next(iter(NVIDIA_DISCOVERABLE))
        result = ModelDiscovery([replacement], [], [replacement], ["취소한 모델 확인의 실패 내용"])
        with patch("frametag.ui.discover_photo_models", return_value=result), \
                patch.object(self.app, "_show_model_verification") as show:
            self.app._fetch_models()
            deadline = time.monotonic() + 1
            while True:
                with self.app.events.mutex:
                    queued = any(name == "models" for name, _ in self.app.events.queue)
                if queued or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    break
                time.sleep(.005)
            self.assertTrue(queued)
            self.app._fetch_models()  # Cancel before Tk consumes the ready result.
            self.wait(lambda: not self.app._models_pending)
            show.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual(self.app.model.get(), original)
        self.assertFalse(self.app._verified_models.get(("NVIDIA", self.app.api_key.get())))
        self.assertEqual(FakeAnalyzer.calls, [])
        self.assertIn("중지", self.app.status.get())

    def test_model_retirement_during_real_analysis_clears_and_persists_selection(self):
        retired = next(iter(NVIDIA_DISCOVERABLE))
        self._verify_model_for_test(retired)
        class RetiredAnalyzer:
            def analyze(_self, path, cancel):
                raise APIError("The selected model has reached its end of life", fatal=True,
                               status_code=410, model_retired=retired)
        with patch.object(self.app.engine, "_factory", return_value=RetiredAnalyzer()):
            self.app._analyze()
            self.wait(lambda: self.app.model.get() == "" and not self.app._busy)
        self.app._save_settings()
        self.assertIn(retired, self.settings.load()["retired_nvidia_models"])
        self.assertNotIn(retired, self.app._verified_models[("NVIDIA", self.app.api_key.get())])
        self.assertTrue(all(photo.path.exists() for photo in self.app.photos))
        self.assertEqual(sum(photo.status == "오류" for photo in self.app.photos), 1)
        self.assertEqual(sum(photo.status == "대기" for photo in self.app.photos), 2)
        self.assertTrue(self.errors)

    def test_api_diagnostic_uses_new_key_and_gui_animates_while_server_waits(self):
        entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
        self.addCleanup(release.set)
        captured = []
        report = "모델 목록 HTTP 200\n분석 경로 HTTP 410\n서버 설명: Inference route disabled"
        def diagnostic(provider, model, key, cancel):
            captured.append((provider, model, key, cancel))
            entered.set()
            release.wait(2)
            return report
        new_key = "mock-diagnostic-new-key-not-valid"
        self.app.api_key.set(new_key)
        with patch("frametag.ui.diagnose_connection", side_effect=diagnostic), \
                patch.object(self.app, "_show_diagnostic") as show:
            start = time.monotonic()
            self.app._diagnose_api()
            self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - start, .25)
            self.wait(entered.is_set)
            self.assertEqual(captured[0][:3], ("NVIDIA", self.app.model.get(), new_key))
            self.assertTrue(self.app._diagnosing)
            self.assertIn("진단 중", self.app.loading_text.get())
            self.assertEqual(str(self.app.progress["mode"]), "indeterminate")
            initial_progress = float(self.app.progress["value"])
            heartbeat = []
            self.app.after(30, lambda: heartbeat.append(True))
            self.wait(lambda: bool(heartbeat), timeout=.5)
            self.wait(lambda: float(self.app.progress["value"]) > initial_progress, timeout=.5)
            self.assertFalse(release.is_set())
            release.set()
            self.wait(lambda: not self.app._diagnosing)
            show.assert_called_once_with(report)
        self.assertEqual(str(self.app.diagnostic_button["state"]), "normal")
        self.assertEqual(self.app.loading_text.get(), "")
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_old_key_discovery_cannot_change_model_after_key_is_replaced(self):
        entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
        self.addCleanup(release.set)
        original_model = self.app.model.get()
        available = list(NVIDIA_DISCOVERABLE)[:1]
        def lookup(provider, key, preferred, cancel, **kwargs):
            entered.set()
            release.wait(2)
            return ModelDiscovery(available, [], available, ["이전 키의 모델 확인 실패"])
        with patch("frametag.ui.discover_photo_models", side_effect=lookup), \
                patch.object(self.app, "_show_model_verification") as show:
            self.app._fetch_models()
            self.wait(entered.is_set)
            self.app.api_key.set("mock-changed-during-discovery")
            release.set()
            self.wait(lambda: not self.app._models_pending)
            show.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual(self.app.model.get(), original_model)
        self.assertIn("중지", self.app.status.get())
        self.assertFalse(self.errors)

    def test_diagnostic_identifies_result_from_settings_changed_during_request(self):
        entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
        self.addCleanup(release.set)
        def diagnostic(*args):
            entered.set()
            release.wait(2)
            return "이전 설정의 진단 결과"
        with patch("frametag.ui.diagnose_connection", side_effect=diagnostic), \
                patch.object(self.app, "_show_diagnostic") as show:

            self.app.api_key.set("mock-changed-during-diagnostic")
